This is complemented by a selective caching strategy that identifies a representative client subset to form the subspace, reducing server memory. Experiments demonstrate that FedSteer significantly outperforms baselines, preventing performance collapse in challenging scenarios while delivering accuracy gains of over 7% in others.

</details>


### [74] [Discovering Interpretable Multi-Parameter Control Policies for Evolutionary Algorithms Using Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10129)
*Tai Nguyen,Phong Le,Carola Doerr,Nguyen Dang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用深度强化学习克服进化算法中多参数控制的理论分析难题，以(1+($λ$,$λ$))-遗传算法优化OneMax问题为例，通过引入动作空间分解、奖励偏移和长视界折扣等算法无关的改进，发现Double Deep Q-Networks能避免策略崩溃，并将所学行为蒸馏为可解释的符号控制策略，该策略不仅可解释性强，且在各种问题规模下均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习已越来越多地应用于进化算法的参数控制，但参数控制的理论分析由于难以推导有效、可解释且适合形式化研究的多参数策略，仍主要局限于单参数设置。本文旨在克服这一障碍，将深度RL用于多参数控制的理论分析。

Method: 以(1+($λ$,$λ$))-遗传算法优化OneMax为案例，首先发现标准方法在多参数设置中难以收敛；引入算法无关的增强方法：动作空间分解、奖励偏移和长视界折扣；比较常见深度RL方法，发现Double Deep Q-Networks能避免PPO中的策略崩溃；将神经网络学到的策略蒸馏为符号化的透明控制策略。

Result: DDQN产生的轨迹适合下游分析；蒸馏得到的符号控制策略不仅可解释，而且在广泛问题规模下持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 深度RL可以克服多参数控制理论分析的障碍，通过适当的增强方法（动作分解、奖励调整、折扣设计）和知识蒸馏，能够从深度RL中提取出透明、高性能的符号策略，为未来进化算法的理论分析提供可解释的基础。

Abstract: While deep Reinforcement Learning (deep-RL) has been increasingly applied to parameter control in evolutionary algorithms, rigorous theoretical analysis of parameter control remains largely restricted to single-parameter settings, owing to the difficulty of deriving effective, interpretable multi-parameter policies amenable to formal study. We demonstrate how deep-RL can be leveraged to overcome this barrier, using the (1+($λ$,$λ$))-genetic algorithm optimizing OneMax, one of the few problems where a super-constant speedup of dynamic control has been formally proven, as a representative case study. We first show that standard approaches struggle to converge in this multi-parameter setting, and introduce algorithm-agnostic enhancements targeting action-space decomposition, reward shifting, and long-horizon discounting. With these in place, we compare common deep-RL methods and find that Double Deep Q-Networks uniquely avoid the policy collapse observed in Proximal Policy Optimization, yielding trajectories suitable for downstream analysis. Crucially, we move beyond the ``black-box'' nature of neural networks by distilling the learned behaviors into a transparent, symbolic control policy. This resulting policy does not only offer interpretability for future theoretical analysis but also yields exceptional performance, consistently outperforming existing baselines across a wide range of problem sizes.

</details>


### [75] [Ambiguous Strategic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10137)
*Ivri Hikri,Nir Rosenfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在战略分类中，系统只被要求披露部分信息（而非完全公开）的情况，并引入鲁棒机制设计中的"模糊性"概念，让学习器可以披露一组可能的分类器范围而非精确分类器，同时私下决定最终实现哪一个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有战略分类研究通常假设分类器完全公开，但现实中监管可能只要求部分披露。这引发了一个问题：系统为何要选择完全披露？在部分披露的监管要求下，学习器需要同时优化分类器及其不确定性。

Method: 采用鲁棒机制设计中的"模糊性"概念，允许学习器披露一组或一个范围的分类器（而非精确值），同时私下选择最终实现哪个分类器。提出了计算最佳响应和训练的高效算法。

Result: 通过实证探索，展示了在这种新设置下战略学习及其结果的表现。

Conclusion: 部分披露（模糊性）是一种可行的策略，在监管要求下系统可以借此优化学习任务，并且本文提供了相应的算法支持。

Abstract: A common assumption in strategic classification is that the classifier is public knowledge. However, it remains unclear whether, and why, a system would choose to commit to full disclosure. We study a setting in which regulation requires the system to disclose some, but not all, of the information. This induces a learning task in which the learner must jointly optimize the classifier and the uncertainty surrounding it. To this end, we adopt from robust mechanism design the notion of ambiguity, which in our setting allows the learner to reveal a set or range of possible classifiers, while privately choosing which of them to ultimately realize. We investigate how ambiguity affects the learning task, develop efficient algorithms for computing best-responses and training, and empirically explore strategic learning and its outcomes in this novel setting and using our approach.

</details>


### [76] [Compositional Generative Modeling from Decentralized Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10153)
*Mashrur M. Morshed,Vishnu Naresh Boddeti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出去中心化组合流匹配（DCFM）框架，通过在不交换原始数据的情况下对全局生成因子施加结构约束，使得分散数据源中的组合知识能够涌现，在条件图像生成、机器人空间规划和医学属性共现建模任务上显著优于联邦学习和混合专家基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据常分散在不同孤岛中，导致物理世界中具有组合性质的生成因子被碎片化隔离。现有去中心化生成方法仅关注建模孤岛数据的并集，忽略了由整体隐含的新颖组合。

Method: 提出DCFM框架，在无需交换原始数据的前提下，对全局生成因子集合施加结构性约束；通过节点间的对等交互实现组合涌现，即使单一数据源无法独立支持该组合。

Result: 在条件图像生成、机器人空间规划、医学属性共现建模三个任务上，DCFM显著优于联邦学习和混合专家基线方法。

Conclusion: DCFM有效解决了去中心化场景下的组合生成问题，证明了通过对全局因子施加结构约束和节点间交互，可以从分散数据中产生新颖的组合知识。

Abstract: Learning the compositional nature of the physical world requires joint observation of interacting factors. However, because practical data is often decentralized, these factors are fragmented across isolated silos. Existing decentralized generative approaches focus only on modeling the union of siloed data, overlooking novel combinations implied by the collective whole. To bridge this gap, we introduce Decentralized Compositional Flow Matching (DCFM), a framework that enforces structural constraints across the global set of generative factors, without exchanging any raw data. DCFM enables novel combinations to emerge through peer interactions, even when no single data source can independently support the composition. Empirically, DCFM substantially outperforms federated learning and mixture-of-experts baselines across conditional image generation, robotic spatial planning, and medical attribute co-occurrence modeling.

</details>


### [77] [Quality Is Not a Safety Proxy Under Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10154)
*Sahil Kadadekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计了量化检查点中常见的"用质量指标替代直接安全测试"的捷径，在涵盖6个模型、4个模型家族、7级GGUF阶梯及AWQ/GPTQ INT4量化方案的51行矩阵中，发现该捷径完全失效：所有36个质量-安全配对在不同模型间方向分裂，存在9个隐藏危险行和1个近隐藏危险行，其中质量稳定或提升但拒绝率下降12-68个百分点。四项机械论探针无法区分危险行，Claude Sonnet 4重新标注验证了结果。研究提出的校准化行为筛查指标RTSI成功将10/10的隐藏/近隐藏危险行导向直接安全测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中的常见做法是先用量化模型的质量指标（如困惑度、任务准确率）筛选检查点，如果质量看起来没问题，就跳过直接的安全测试。这种"捷径"是否可靠？本文旨在系统性地审计这一做法。

Method: 构建了一个51行的矩阵，涵盖6个模型、4个模型家族、7级GGUF量化阶梯以及AWQ/GPTQ INT4量化方案。对每一行测量质量指标和安全指标（拒绝率），分析质量-安全之间的关系。同时进行四项机械论探针（熵、拒绝方向、校准探针、神经元量化误差吸收分析），并设计了Refusal Template Stability Index (RTSI)进行校准化行为筛查。

Result: 质量-安全配对在所有模型间方向分裂（36个配对无一致方向）；9个隐藏危险行和1个近隐藏危险行显示质量稳定或提升但安全显著下降（拒绝率下降12-68个百分点）；11个AWQ/GPTQ行中有7个是隐藏危险；四项机械论探针均无法有效区分危险行；RTSI成功识别10/10危险行，在匹配桶大小下比最佳单特征基线（独特前缀率差、原始拒绝率差）表现更好（9/10和8/10）。

Conclusion: 对于所研究的量化检查点、模型家族和安全结果，保留的质量不能替代直接的安全评估。质量指标通过不能作为跳过安全测试的充分理由。

Abstract: Quantized checkpoints are often screened first with quality metrics and only later, if at all, with direct safety tests. This paper audits that shortcut on a matched 51-row matrix spanning 6 models, 4 families, a 7-level GGUF ladder, and AWQ/GPTQ INT4 checkpoints. In this matrix the shortcut fails: all 36 quality-safety pairings split direction across models, and 9 hidden-danger rows plus 1 near-hidden-danger row show quality stable or improved while refusal falls by 12-68 percentage points. Seven of the 11 AWQ/GPTQ rows are hidden-danger. A four-probe mechanistic follow-up over the 17 Hugging Face-backed FP16/AWQ/GPTQ cells does not rescue it: entropy, refusal-direction, and calibration probes are weak or null separators of dangerous rows, and although probe-identified safety-associated neurons absorb 1.39$\times$ more quantization error overall ($p < 5 \times 10^{-7}$), the effect is not regime-specific. Claude Sonnet 4 relabels 11,470 items in a predefined stratified set, agrees with the primary gemma3:12b judge on 89.9\% of rows ($κ= 0.873$, 95\% CI [0.866, 0.881]), and changes 0/10 hidden-danger cells. A calibrated study-internal behavioral screen -- the Refusal Template Stability Index (RTSI), built from four refusal-template drift features and calibrated on this matrix -- routes 10/10 hidden- or near-hidden-danger rows to direct safety testing (Wilson 95\% CI lower bound 0.72) while leaving 23 of 45 non-baseline rows in a low-risk bucket under both in-sample scoring and row-level leave-one-out validation; on the same matrix, the best single-feature baselines (unique-prefix-rate-delta, raw refusal-rate delta) recover 9/10 and 8/10 respectively at matched bucket size, and cross-stack transfer requires recalibration. For the quantized checkpoints, model families, and safety outcomes studied here, retained quality cannot waive direct safety evaluation.

</details>


### [78] [Learning Entropy and Spatial Adaptation Dynamics of Multilayer Perceptrons for Structural Point Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10170)
*Jan Glaser,Ivo Bukovsky,Marcel Jirina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将学习熵（LE）从时间自适应系统扩展到空间学习领域，提出空间学习熵图（SLEM）方法，通过训练MLP根据周围上下文预测中心像素强度，评估学习过程中神经网络权重的增量适应，从而识别出对学习过程有重要影响的异常图像点和区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像特征提取和可解释性方法（如局部邻域方法）直接评估图像结构（梯度或协方差算子），缺乏从学习过程本身出发的视角。本文旨在通过分析神经网络在学习过程中的适应性变化，提供一种互补的图像分析范式。

Method: 训练MLP根据周围空间上下文预测中心像素强度，从图像衍生样本的学习过程中评估神经网络权重的增量适应（即学习熵），生成空间学习熵图（SLEM）以标识引发强网络适应的异常点和区域。

Result: 空间学习熵提供了一种与传统特征提取和可解释性方法互补的视角，能够根据学习影响而非局部结构属性标识图像点和区域，突出对网络学习特别具有信息量的空间位置。

Conclusion: 该方法为计算机视觉、制造和机器人领域的基于学习的图像或场景分析开辟了新方向。

Abstract: This paper extends the concept of Learning Entropy (LE) from temporal adaptive systems to spatial learning in multilayer perceptron networks (MLPs) applied to image data. Instead of evaluating image structure directly from gradients or covariance operators, as local neighborhood methods do, the proposed approach analyzes the learning process itself through Learning Entropy. An MLP is trained to predict the intensity of a center pixel from its surrounding spatial context, while LE is evaluated from the incremental adaptation of neural weights during learning across image-derived samples. The resulting Spatial Learning Entropy Maps (SLEM) identify unusual image points and regions that induce strong adaptation of the neural network and therefore have an important role in the learning process. The results indicate that spatial Learning Entropy provides a complementary perspective to conventional feature extraction and explainability methods by highlighting spatial locations that are particularly informative for network learning. Spatial Learning Entropy provides a complementary perspective to conventional feature extraction and explainability methods by identifying image points and regions according to their learning impact rather than their local structural properties. The proposed framework may open new directions for learning-driven image or scene analysis in computer vision, manufacturing, and robotics.

</details>


### [79] [Dropout-GRPO: Variational Stochasticity for Continuous Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10184)
*Wooil Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO在连续潜在推理模型（如Coconut）中因确定性轨迹导致策略梯度失效的问题，本文提出通过结构化Dropout（每个rollout使用固定的Bernoulli掩码）引入随机性，使GRPO能优化贝叶斯模型平均策略的期望奖励。在GSM8K上，该方法将Coconut基线从27.29%提升至29.01% pass@1，并提供了理论保证（无偏性、方差缩减、潜在梯度良定义性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO依赖组内K个rollout的多样性计算组均值优势，但Coconut等潜在推理模型的隐状态在给定参数和提示下是确定性的，导致多个rollout产生相同轨迹，优势函数归零，使得GRPO无法学习。

Method: 对每个rollout施加一个在所有潜在递归步骤中保持不变的Bernoulli掩码（结构化Dropout），从而在连续隐空间中引入轨迹方差。每个rollout被视作参数变分分布的后验样本，GRPO优化的是贝叶斯模型平均策略的期望奖励。

Result: 在GSM8K数据集上，dropout-GRPO将Coconut基线从27.29%提升至29.01% pass@1，验证了GRPO在潜在推理模型上的可行性。

Conclusion: 结构化Dropout为潜在推理模型的后训练提供了一种实用且理论完备的GRPO学习方法，包括无偏性、方差缩减和潜在梯度良定义性的理论证明。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) relies on the diversity of $K$ rollouts within each group; otherwise, the group-mean advantage $A^{(k)} = r^{(k)} - μ_r$ collapses to zero. This presents a structural challenge for latent-reasoning models like Coconut, which feed continuous hidden states recurrently in place of discrete chain-of-thought tokens. Because the latent phase is inherently deterministic given the parameters and prompt, multiple rollouts produce identical trajectories, stalling GRPO's progress. Consequently, applying group-relative reinforcement learning to continuous latent reasoning has proven difficult.
  To address this, we propose sourcing the necessary stochasticity through structured dropout. By applying a single Bernoulli mask held constant across all latent recurrence steps for a given rollout, we generate essential trajectory variance. This shared mask effectively treats each rollout as a posterior sample from a variational distribution over parameters, allowing GRPO to optimize the expected reward of a Bayesian model-average policy. We provide both theoretical justification for this method -- including unbiasedness, variance reduction, and the well-definedness of the latent gradient -- and empirical validation. On GSM8K, dropout-GRPO improves a Coconut baseline from $27.29\%$ to $29.01\%$ pass@1, demonstrating the viability of GRPO learning for latent-reasoning models. Our work positions this as a practical, theoretically grounded approach for post-training latent-reasoning LLMs.

</details>


### [80] [MMClima: A Framework for Multimodal Climate Science Data and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10194)
*Muhammad Umer Sheikh,Hassan Abid,Khawar Shehzad,Ufaq Khan,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MMClima是一个包含10.4万+专家验证问答对的大规模多模态气候问答框架，覆盖文本、视频转录和图表，用于评估多模态语言模型在气候科学领域的推理能力，并发布了数据集、评估流水线和微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有气候QA基准规模小、主要以文本为主，且覆盖模型范围窄，无法满足气候变化研究对跨文本、动态视觉内容和科学图表推理的需求。

Method: 通过自动化的声明提取和QA合成，结合人工验证的闭环流程构建大规模多模态数据集；在文本分片上微调得到mmclima-70b-txt领域适应基线模型。

Result: 构建了104k+专家验证的问答对；微调模型mmclima-70b-txt在文本QA上优于强开源和闭源模型。

Conclusion: MMClima为气候科学提供了标准化多模态评估基准，推动了气候AI系统在跨模态推理方面的发展。

Abstract: Climate change research increasingly requires AI systems that reason across text, dynamic visual content, and scientific figures, yet existing climate QA benchmarks are small, mostly textual, and cover a narrow range of models. We introduce MMClima, a large-scale multimodal climate question answering framework with 104k+ expert-validated question-answer pairs spanning articles, video transcriptions, and figures across five core climate science domains. MMClima is constructed via automated claim extraction and QA synthesis with human-in-the-loop validation to ensure both scale and reliability. Using MMClima, we benchmark state-of-the-art multimodal language models on tasks requiring factual recall, visual interpretation, and cross-modal synthesis. We additionally fine-tune on the textual split to produce mmclima-70b-txt, a domain-adapted baseline that outperforms strong open- and closed-source models on textual QA. We release the dataset, evaluation pipeline, fine-tuned model weights, and data creation framework to support standardized multimodal evaluation for climate science.

</details>


### [81] [Density Ridge Selective Prediction for LLM and VLM Hallucination Detection under Calibration Label Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10198)
*Nina I. Shamsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于密度脊的幻觉检测方法，利用六维运动学特征映射和核密度估计构建LLM响应流形，通过到最近脊顶点的负欧氏距离评分，在标签稀缺场景下AUROC提升5-20个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督采样检测器性能饱和，有监督探针在标定标签稀缺时性能急剧下降，需要一种既避免标签依赖又保持鲁棒性的幻觉检测方法。

Method: 1) 提取隐藏状态生成轨迹的六维运动学特征映射；2) 基于核密度估计构建密度脊作为响应流形；3) 将测试生成投影到特征空间，计算其到最近脊顶点的负欧氏距离作为置信度评分。

Result: 在7个QA基准、9个文本/视觉LLM上的标签稀缺协议（200条标定查询，5次生成）中，AUROC提升5-20个点，且标定标签稀缺时性能退化更温和。

Conclusion: 基于密度脊的几何骨架方法在标签稀缺场景下显著优于现有幻觉检测方法，兼具无监督方法的免标注特性和有监督方法的强判别力。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language and vision-language models is increasingly framed as selective prediction, where a detector assigns a confidence score and abstains when confidence is low. Unsupervised sampling detectors (Semantic Entropy, EigenScore) avoid labels but plateau in quality, while supervised probes (SAPLMA) attain stronger in-distribution scores yet degrade sharply when calibration labels are scarce. We recover the response manifold of an LLM as the density ridge of a kernel density estimate built on a six-dimensional kinematic feature map of hidden state generation trajectories. A test generation is scored by the negated Euclidean distance from its projected feature point to the nearest ridge vertex, yielding a low-dimensional geometric skeleton of the stochastic output distribution. We evaluate against Semantic Entropy, SAR, EigenScore, SAPLMA, and log-probability on seven QA benchmarks (HaluEval-QA, TriviaQA, GSM8K, POPE, ScienceQA, A-OKVQA) using nine text and vision LLMs in a deliberately label-scarce protocol ($n_{\text{cal}}{=}200$ queries, $N{=}5$ generations). Our ridge-based score beats on AUROC with 5-20 points gain, while demonstrating tempered degradation under calibration-label scarcity.

</details>


### [82] [A Continuous-Time Markov Chain Framework for Insertion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10199)
*Dhruvesh Patel,Benjamin Rozonoyer,Soumitra Das,Tahira Naseem,Tim G. J. Rudner,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，将插入式语言模型（ILM）的加噪过程建模为变长序列空间上的连续时间马尔可夫链，推导出扩散风格的去噪目标函数，证明现有ILM公式是其特例，并在合成规划任务和语言建模中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有插入式生成方法的公式化定义大多是启发式的（ad-hoc），缺乏统一的、从第一性原理出发的理论推导框架。

Method: 将加噪过程形式化为变长序列空间上的连续时间马尔可夫链，基于此推导出扩散风格的去噪目标函数。

Result: 在合成规划任务上，该方法保留了插入式生成相对于自左向右生成和掩码扩散模型的优势；在语言建模任务上，该方法与自左向右生成和掩码扩散模型竞争力相当，同时比现有插入式语言模型提供更灵活的采样能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于扩散的去噪框架统一了现有的插入式语言模型公式，且在实践中兼具插入式生成的优势和扩散模型的灵活性。

Abstract: Insertion Language Models (ILMs) offer several advantages over left-to-right generation and mask-based generation. However, existing formulations of insertion-based generation have largely been ad-hoc. In this paper, we derive a diffusion-style denoising objective for ILMs from first principles by formulating the noising process as a continuous-time Markov chain on the space of variable-length sequences. We show that previous formulations of ILMs can be viewed as special cases of this denoising framework. Through empirical evaluation on a synthetic planning task, we show that the proposed approach retains the benefits of insertion-based generation over left-to-right generation and masked diffusion models. In language modeling, our diffusion-based approach is competitive with left-to-right generation and masked diffusion models, while offering additional flexibility in sampling compared to existing insertion language models.

</details>


### [83] [A Source Domain is All You Need: Source-Only Cross-OS Transfer Learning for APT Anomaly Detection via Semantic Alignment and Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10216)
*Sidahmed Benabderrahmanea,Petko Valtchev,James Cheney,Talal Rahwan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于最优传输的源域无监督跨操作系统APT检测框架，通过将系统溯源数据抽象为自然语言描述、利用预训练语言模型嵌入，并结合语义、结构和几何三个异常通道，在无目标域标签的情况下实现跨平台APT检测。在DARPA数据集上的实验表明该方法优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT检测面临标记数据稀缺、类别严重不平衡、恶意行为生成困难等挑战，这些问题在跨操作系统场景下更为突出——检测器在源平台训练后需部署到无标签的目标平台上。现有方法难以在无目标域监督下实现有效的跨OS APT检测。

Method: 提出基于最优传输的框架：1) 将进程行为抽象为结构化自然语言描述，用预训练语言模型嵌入；2) 结合三种证据通道——语义偏差（源域正常原型偏离）、结构偏差（图自编码）和几何偏差（最优传输）；3) 核心创新是基于OT的质心异常分数，将目标嵌入投影到源域正常流形并量化传输残差；4) 引入熵加权、角度感知和密度感知的OT变体。

Result: 在DARPA Transparent Computing数据集上（Linux、Windows、BSD、Android），覆盖两个APT场景和12个跨OS迁移对，所提框架在ROC-AUC和nDCG指标上优于源域无监督异常检测基线。

Conclusion: 源域无监督的溯源建模，结合语义抽象和基于最优传输的异常评分，能够在无目标域监督的条件下支持实用的跨平台APT检测。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) are stealthy, multi-stage cyberattacks whose detection is difficult due to scarce labeled traces, severe class imbalance, and the challenge of generating realistic malicious behavior. These challenges are amplified in cross-operating-system (cross-OS) settings, where a detector trained on one source platform must be deployed on an unlabeled target platform without access to target-domain labels. We study this source-only cross-OS APT detection problem using system-level provenance traces and propose a transport-based framework for ranking anomalous target processes under zero target supervision. The framework abstracts process behavior into structured natural-language descriptions, embeds them using pretrained language models, and constructs a source-normal reference for target scoring. It combines three evidence channels: semantic deviation from source-normal prototypes, structural deviation captured by graph autoencoding, and geometric deviation measured through Optimal Transport (OT). The main contribution is an OT-based barycentric anomaly score that projects target embeddings onto the source-normal manifold and quantifies residual transport mismatch. We further introduce entropy-weighted, angle-aware, and density-aware OT variants to capture uncertainty, directional drift, and sparse-support behavior. Evaluation on DARPA Transparent Computing data spanning Linux, Windows, BSD, and Android, across two APT scenarios and twelve cross-OS transfer pairs, shows that the proposed framework improves ROC-AUC and nDCG over source-only anomaly-detection baselines. The results demonstrate that source-only provenance modeling, combined with semantic abstraction and OT-based anomaly scoring, can support practical cross-platform APT detection without target-domain supervision.

</details>


### [84] [Alignment Defends LLMs from Property Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10217)
*Pengrun Huang,Chhavi Yadav,Ruihan Wu,Kamalika Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对LLM属性推理攻击，提出基于对齐的后训练防御方法（DPO和GRPO），在不修改训练数据的情况下重塑模型输出分布，有效缓解攻击并保持良好效用-机密性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法需要访问原始数据并重新训练模型，限制了在数据不可用或模型已部署场景下的适用性。

Method: 基于对齐的后训练防御，通过构建偏好对（DPO）和定义特定奖励函数（GRPO）重塑模型输出分布至目标属性比率，无需修改训练数据。

Result: 实验表明该方法有效缓解属性推理攻击，同时保持较强的效用-机密性权衡。

Conclusion: 基于对齐的防御方法为LLM属性隐私保护提供了无需数据访问和重新训练的有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly fine-tuned on domain-specific datasets that may contain sensitive, dataset-level properties. Recent work has shown that such dataset-level information can be effectively extracted through property inference attacks, posing a confidentiality risk. Existing defenses against these attacks primarily operate by modifying the training data distribution and hence require access to the original data and retraining the model, limiting their applicability to settings where data is unavailable or models are already deployed. In this work, we propose alignment-based defenses for mitigating property inference attacks in LLMs. Our approach reshapes the model's output distribution towards a target property ratio via post-training alignment, without modifying the training data. In particular, we adapt two widely used RLHF frameworks--Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)--as our defenses by constructing preference pairs and defining a specific reward function respectively. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that our alignment based defenses effectively mitigate property inference attacks while maintaining a strong utility confidentiality tradeoff.

</details>


### [85] [Fast Exact Nearest-Neighbor Learning for High-Frequency Financial Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10219)
*Henry Han,Diane Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Mojo语言的SIMD k-d树方法，用于高频金融时间序列的精确最近邻学习。在八个金融数据集上，相比scikit-learn的k-d树实现x86加速17.5-21.6倍，ARM64加速28.1-43.5倍，同时保持精确输出。该方法还支持基于Extra Trees的隐含波动率定价模型，训练数据量提升10倍，put-IV RMSE降低8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融领域市场数据量激增（涵盖股票、ETF、外汇、期权、高频交易流），成熟的金融AI系统面临核心挑战：模型需从更大规模的历史语料库中学习，同时满足实时交易、风险管理和衍生品定价的延迟约束。

Method: 提出Mojo SIMD k-d树，包含三个关键技术：基于方差的节点分裂策略、连续扁平缓冲区存储、编译期向量化距离计算。以精确最近邻学习作为具体案例，在固定股票、大样本量、中等维度的场景下进行分析。

Result: 理论上，在标准剪枝和实现成本假设下，Mojo SIMD k-d树在固定股票、大n、中等维度场景下渐近优于Mojo SIMD暴力搜索和scikit-learn k-d树。实验上，在x86上比scikit-learn k-d树快17.5-21.6倍，在ARM64的股票/ETF数据集上比scikit-learn暴力搜索快28.1-43.5倍。Extra Trees模型训练数据量提升10倍，put-IV RMSE降低8.0%。

Conclusion: Mojo可作为一个可扩展、生产就绪的金融AI技术栈，同时也是其他数据密集型领域高效AI的有前景的基础。

Abstract: AI efficiency at scale is becoming critical in finance as market data volumes surge across equities, ETFs, FX, options, and high-frequency trading streams. This growth creates a core challenge for mature financial AI systems: models must learn from larger historical corpora while still meeting real-time latency constraints in trading, risk management, and derivative pricing. We use exact nearest-neighbor learning for high-frequency financial time series as a concrete case study to show that Mojo-based financial AI can address this challenge. We introduce a Mojo SIMD k-d tree with variance-based splitting, contiguous flat-buffer storage, and compile-time vectorized distance computation. We also provide a runtime result showing that, under standard pruning and implementation-cost assumptions, the Mojo SIMD k-d tree asymptotically dominates Mojo SIMD brute force and scikit-learn's k-d tree in the fixed-stock, large-$n$, moderate-dimensional regime. Empirically, across eight financial datasets on x86 and ARM64 with up to 277K training samples, the method achieves 17.5--21.6$\times$ speedup over scikit-learn's k-d tree on x86 and 28.1--43.5$\times$ over scikit-learn brute force on ARM64 equity/ETF datasets, while preserving exact outputs. Beyond nearest-neighbor inference, Mojo's compiled execution enables an Extra Trees-based implied-volatility pricing model to train on $10\times$ more options data, reducing put-IV RMSE by 8.0\%. These results position Mojo as a scalable, production-ready stack for financial AI and a promising foundation for efficient AI in other data-intensive fields. \keywords{Financial AI \and AI Efficiency \and Mojo \and SIMD \and K-D Trees \and KNN \and High-Frequency Trading \and Financial Time Series \and Scaling}

</details>


### [86] [Spatiotemporal Graph Transformer for 3D Neighborhood Interaction and Quality Prediction in Metal Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10227)
*Joyce Karen Pelaez,Siqi Zhang,Hoo Sang Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种时空图Transformer，用于建模金属增材制造中的3D邻域交互并学习其对构建质量的影响，通过加权网络表示融合制造过程和多模态数据，并设计双注意力图Transformer捕获节点内特征依赖和跨节点邻域交互，实验表明该方法显著优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属增材制造中重复的逐层熔化、凝固和再加热过程导致层间交互作用复杂，现有方法难以有效表示多层交互及其对质量的贡献，亟需一种能够建模3D邻域交互并量化其质量影响的方法。

Method: 首先构建加权网络表示制造过程，将熔融位置建模为节点，空间和过程依赖关系编码为边权重，并集成多模态数据；在此基础上设计双注意力图Transformer，同时捕获节点内特征依赖和跨节点邻域交互进行质量表示学习。

Result: 所提框架在表征工艺-质量关系方面显著优于基于图像、序列和图的方法，跨层交互的引入对提升质量预测性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 该方法可广泛应用于涉及网络建模和基于图的表示学习的其他任务。

Abstract: Metal additive manufacturing enables the fabrication of complex parts, but achieving consistent build quality remains challenging due to interactions induced by repeated layer-wise melting, solidification, and reheating across the 3D build. Advanced sensing provide a great opportunity to collect rich observations of the actual manufacturing process for real-time quality monitoring and control. Yet, existing methods often have limited ability to represent multi-layer interactions and quantify their contributions to quality. In this paper, we develop a novel spatiotemporal graph transformer for modeling 3D neighborhood interactions and learn their effects on build quality in metal additive manufacturing. Specifically, we first introduce a weighted network representation of the manufacturing process, where fusing locations are modeled as nodes, and their spatial- and process-dependent relationships are encoded as edge weights. This representation also enables the integration of multimodal data (e.g., geometric design, process settings, and in-situ sensing data) into a unified structure for downstream learning tasks. Building on this network, we further design a dual-attention graph transformer that captures both within-node feature dependencies and cross-node neighborhood interactions for quality representation learning. Experimental results show that the proposed framework significantly outperforms image-based, sequence-based, and graph-based models in characterizing process-quality relationships. More importantly, the incorporation of cross-layer interactions is critical for improving quality prediction performance. This framework is broadly applicable to other tasks involving network modeling and graph-based representation learning.

</details>


### [87] [SHAPO: Sharpness-Aware Policy Optimization for Safe Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10228)
*Kaustubh Mani,Yann Pequignot,Vincent Mai,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHAPO提出了一种基于锐度感知的策略优化方法，通过评估扰动参数处的梯度来实现保守的、不确定性感知的安全探索，在连续控制任务中显著提升了安全性与任务性能的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键领域部署强化学习智能体需要安全探索，而现有方法在平衡探索安全性与任务性能方面仍有不足。

Method: 提出Sharpness-Aware Policy Optimization (SHAPO)，利用锐度感知策略更新规则，在扰动参数处评估梯度，使策略更新对智能体的认知不确定性保持悲观；从理论上证明该调整隐含地重新加权策略梯度，放大罕见不安全动作的影响，抑制已安全动作的贡献。

Result: 在多个连续控制任务上，SHAPO在安全性和任务性能上均优于现有基线，显著扩展了帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 通过认知不确定性驱动的悲观策略更新，SHAPO有效地实现了安全探索，证明了锐度感知机制在安全RL中的有效性。

Abstract: Safe exploration is a prerequisite for deploying reinforcement learning (RL) agents in safety-critical domains. In this paper, we approach safe exploration through the lens of epistemic uncertainty, where the actor's sensitivity to parameter perturbations serves as a practical proxy for regions of high uncertainty. We propose Sharpness-Aware Policy Optimization (SHAPO), a sharpness-aware policy update rule that evaluates gradients at perturbed parameters, making policy updates pessimistic with respect to the actor's epistemic uncertainty. Analytically we show that this adjustment implicitly reweighs policy gradients, amplifying the influence of rare unsafe actions while tempering contributions from already safe ones, thereby biasing learning toward conservative behavior in under-explored regions. Across several continuous-control tasks, our method consistently improves both safety and task performance over existing baselines, significantly expanding their Pareto frontiers.

</details>


### [88] [DUET -- Dual User Embedding Transformers for Offsite Conversion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10243)
*Reazul Hasan Russel,Mingwei Tang,Rostam Shirani,Xinlong Liu,Navid Madani,Leo Ding,Yawen He,Xiangyu Wang,Mustafa Acar,Ashish Katiyar,Yuhai Li,Alan Yang,Metarya Ruparel,Derek Qiang Xu,Rupert Wu,Rui Yang,Liang Tao,Xinyi Zhao,Larry Zhang,Sri Reddy,Rob Malkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DUET（双用户嵌入Transformer）框架，针对推荐系统中离线转化率（OCVR）预测任务，将用户行为数据显式划分为点击流和转化流，分别使用适配其统计特性的专用Transformer编码器进行预训练——多层自注意力用于密集点击流，交叉与自注意力交替用于稀疏转化流。生成的互补嵌入由下游排序器联合消费，在不超出服务延迟预算的前提下，相比最强基线实现了最高0.38%的归一化熵（NE）降低，A/B测试也显示OCVR预测精度持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线转化率（OCVR）预测是推荐系统排序中的重要问题，面临点击信号丰富但转化信号稀疏、长延迟且常无法归因的统计异质性挑战。现有预训练方法对两种数据流使用单一无差别的编码器，未能有效处理这种异质性。

Method: 提出DUET框架，将用户行为数据显式分为点击和转化两个领域一致的数据流，分别预训练专用Transformer编码器：点击流使用多层自注意力，转化流使用交叉注意力和自注意力交替的结构。生成的互补嵌入由下游排序器联合消费，且不超出服务延迟预算。

Result: 相比最强基线，DUET实现了最高0.38%的归一化熵（NE）降低；A/B测试显示OCVR预测精度持续改进。

Conclusion: DUET通过为不同统计特性的数据流设计专用编码器，有效解决了点击与转化信号的异质性挑战，在OCVR预测任务上取得了显著且一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Offsite conversion rate (OCVR) prediction is an important ranking problem in computational recommendation systems. This task presents a modeling challenge: click signals are abundant and exhibit short temporal horizons, whereas conversion signals are inherently sparse, long-delayed, and frequently unattributed. Despite these statistical disparities, both signal types must inform models that operate within strict serving-latency constraints. Prior pre-training approaches address this heterogeneity with a single, undifferentiated encoder applied uniformly across both data streams. We propose DUET (Dual User Embedding Transformers), a framework that explicitly partitions user behavioral data into two domain-coherent streams -- clicks and conversions -- and pre-trains dedicated transformer encoders with architectures tailored to each stream's statistical characteristics: multi-layer self-attention for the dense click stream and interleaved cross- and self-attention for the sparse conversion stream. The resulting complementary embeddings are jointly consumed by a downstream ranker without exceeding serving-latency budgets. Evaluation demonstrates up to 0.38% normalized entropy (NE) reduction relative to the strongest baseline, and A/B test shows consistent improvements in OCVR prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [89] [When Design Rules Break: Benchmark Composition Determines Whether Label Informativeness Predicts GNN Aggregator Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10249)
*Neha Sharma,Ritesh Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了GNN中聚合器选择（sum/mean/max）在24个节点分类数据集上的泛化能力，发现边同质性对聚合器性能差异预测较弱，标签信息量在传统基准上有效但在Facebook-100图上失效。谱间隙是区分低信息量数据集的关键指标，PNA在标准引文基准上可能不如单聚合器GIN。基准组成而非数值不足决定了设计规则是否泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GNN设计规则（尤其是聚合器选择）是否能在不同基准家族间泛化，检验现有结论的普遍性，并揭示哪些图特性真正决定聚合器性能差异。

Method: 在24个节点分类数据集（涵盖引文、异质、LINKX Facebook-100、共购买、共作者）上系统比较sum/mean/max聚合器；分析边同质性、标签信息量、谱间隙等图统计量与聚合器性能的关系；使用随机块模型及其度校正变体进行消融实验。

Result: 边同质性对GIN-Sum与GIN-Mean性能差距预测能力弱；标签信息量在传统基准上预测良好，但引入Facebook-100后显著退化；Facebook-100中近零标签信息量与sum聚合偏好共存，增益达7-10%（扩展训练下最高13%）；SBM消融无法复现该现象；谱间隙是唯一能区分这些图的标签无关统计量，效应局限于一跳邻域且跨架构可复现；PNA在标准引文基准上可能不如最佳单聚合器GIN。

Conclusion: GNN设计规则的泛化性取决于基准组成而非样本量不足；Facebook-100图族为未来自适应聚合方法提供了明确的目标场景。

Abstract: We examine whether graph neural network (GNN) design rules generalize across benchmark families by studying aggregator selection (sum, mean, max) on 24 node-classification datasets spanning citation, heterophilic, LINKX Facebook-100, co-purchase, and co-authorship graphs. Edge homophily is only weakly predictive of the GIN-Sum versus GIN-Mean performance gap. Label informativeness predicts this gap well on legacy benchmarks but degrades substantially when Facebook-100 graphs are included. In these dense friendship networks, near-zero label informativeness coexists with a strong preference for sum aggregation, producing gains of 7-10% and up to 13% under extended training. Stochastic block model ablations, including degree-corrected variants matching Facebook-100 degree scales, fail to reproduce this behavior, indicating that mean degree alone does not explain the effect. Among several label-independent graph statistics, the spectral gap uniquely distinguishes these graphs from other low-informativeness datasets, with the effect localized to one-hop neighborhoods and replicated across architectures. We further identify training regimes that interact with aggregator choice and show that PNA can underperform the best single-aggregator GIN on standard citation benchmarks. Our results suggest that benchmark composition, rather than numerical insufficiency, determines whether design rules appear to generalize, and that the Facebook-100 regime provides a concrete target for future adaptive aggregation methods.

</details>


### [90] [Multi-Level Analyzation of Imbalance to Resolve Non-IID-Ness in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10250)
*Haengbok Chung,Jae Sung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedBB通过三级不平衡分析（病例间、类间、客户端间）和两项核心技术（PNB损失函数和CBR客户端重加权）解决了联邦学习中的数据不平衡问题，在X光和自然图像数据集上表现优于其他算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中类别不平衡是导致数据非独立同分布（non-IID）的关键因素，严重损害模型性能。现有方法对不平衡问题的定义和分析不够系统，需要一个全面的解决方案。

Method: 提出FedBB框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) PNB损失函数，通过给少数例或类分配更高权重，同时优化多标签和多类别分类，解决本地训练中的病例间和类间不平衡；(2) CBR客户端重加权，在模型聚合时根据客户端间不平衡程度给模型赋予不同权重，倾斜较小的数据集获得更大权重。

Result: 在X光和自然图像数据集上的实验表明，FedBB在性能和效率上均优于其他算法，且仅需有限的统计信息，有利于隐私保护。消融研究证实PNB损失和CBR各自独立地贡献于性能提升。

Conclusion: FedBB旨在构建能准确分类所有类别的全局模型，可作为通用和个性化联邦学习的基线方法。

Abstract: Class imbalance is a common problem in deep learning that severely degrades performance. In federated learning (FL), it is a critical factor contributing to non-identically distributed data (non-IID). Building on several previous attempts, we define and analyze imbalance issues in FL at three levels: inter-case, inter-class, and inter-client. Inter-case imbalance addresses the imbalance in every single class; inter-class imbalance compares the number of data between different classes. Inter-client imbalance represents different skewness of local data between clients. Based on these concepts, we propose FedBB, which consists of two main components: (1) Positive Negative Balanced (PNB) loss function addresses the inter-case and inter-class imbalances in local training, enhancing generalization on highly skewed local client datasets. It optimizes both multi-label and multi-class classifications by assigning higher weights to minority cases or classes. (2) Client Balanced Reweighting (CBR) reweights clients based on inter-client imbalance during model aggregation, giving greater weight to models trained on less skewed datasets. Various experiments on X-ray and natural image datasets demonstrate that FedBB outperforms other algorithms in both performance and efficiency. Additionally, it requires limited statistical information, which is beneficial for privacy protection. Through ablation studies, we proved that PNB loss and CBR independently contribute to performance. As FedBB aims to build a global model that accurately classifies all classes, it can serve as a baseline for the generic and personalized FL.

</details>


### [91] [A Unified Adaptive Feature Composition Framework for Multi-Task Generalization in Wireless Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10277)
*Yuxuan Shi,Tingting Yang,Kangning Ma,Liwen Jing,Yuwei Wang,Mengfan Zheng,Li Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RAFC路由适配器框架，通过轻量级特征组合网络自适应聚合Transformer各层隐藏状态，实现无线基础模型的多任务泛化，仅需不到50K额外参数即可在四个无线任务上超越传统微调方法，并具备可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线基础模型在下游任务适配中存在两难困境：全参数微调带来巨大计算和存储开销，而冻结特征提取又导致跨任务性能次优。

Method: 提出统一自适应特征组合框架RAFC，将Transformer各层隐藏状态视为多级特征池，通过轻量级任务驱动特征组合网络生成逐层聚合权重，对层次化表示进行加权求和，仅需不到50K额外参数。

Result: 在四个代表性无线任务上，RAFC一致优于传统适配基线，且引入参数量少于5万；学习到的路由权重提供了任务特定层偏好的可解释性证据。

Conclusion: RAFC是一种低复杂度、可扩展且可解释的无线基础模型适配接口，能有效解决多任务泛化问题。

Abstract: Though wireless foundation models (WFMs) have shown strong potential in learning universal channel representations, their adaptation to various downstream tasks remains constrained by existing paradigms. Fine-tuning strategies introduces substantial computational and storage overhead, while frozen feature extraction leads to sub-optimal performance across diverse downstream tasks. To address this issue, we propose a unified adaptive feature composition framework for multitask generalization in WFMs, where the key component is the Routing Adapter for Feature Composition (RAFC). Instead of extracting only the final-layer output, this router treats the hidden states from different Transformer depths as a reusable pool of multi-level hidden features, and employs a lightweight task-driven feature composition network to generate layer-wise aggregation weights, then adaptively combine hierarchical representations through weighted summation. This design enables each downstream task to access suitable mixture of low-, mid-, and high-level wireless features without modifying the pretrained backbone. Extensive experiments on four representative wireless tasks demonstrate that RAFC consistently outperforms conventional adaptation baselines while introducing fewer than 50K additional parameters. Moreover, the learned routing weights provide interpretable evidence of task-specific layer preferences, making the proposed framework a low-complexity, scalable, and explainable interface for adapting WFMs to diverse downstream scenarios.

</details>


### [92] [Revisiting Positive Samples in Graph Contrastive Learning: From the Perspective of Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10284)
*Lianze Shan,Ningchong Wang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现图对比学习（GCL）即使没有正样本也能取得不错性能，从Dirichlet能量角度揭示了消息传递机制导致正样本最大化被平凡化的问题，并提出SPGCL方法：仅传播高Dirichlet能量特征以提供有效学习信号，利用低能量特征构建概率矩阵进行可靠正采样，实验证明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到GCL在无正样本时仍具竞争力，质疑正样本在GCL中的根本作用机制，希望揭示正样本被平凡化的原因并恢复其学习效能。

Method: 从Dirichlet能量视角理论分析消息传递导致正样本最大化被平凡化；提出SPGCL，分离高/低Dirichlet能量特征：高能量特征用于传播以提供有效正学习信号，低能量特征用于构建概率矩阵实现可靠正采样。

Result: 实验结果表明SPGCL能有效提升GCL性能，恢复正样本的学习效能。

Conclusion: 消息传递机制是导致GCL中正样本学习被平凡化的关键因素，通过区分高低Dirichlet能量特征并分别用于传播和采样可有效解决该问题。

Abstract: Graph Contrastive Learning (GCL), which trains graph encoders by maximizing similarity between positive samples and minimizing it between negative ones, has emerged as a mainstream graph pre-training paradigm. It is widely recognized that positive samples are essential in GCLs. Ideally, maximizing the similarity of positive samples enables graph encoders to capture intrinsic semantic and patterns of graph data. However, we discover an interesting phenomenon: GCLs can achieve competitive performance even without positive samples. This motivates us to revisit the fundamental mechanism of positive samples in GCLs. From the perspective of Dirichlet energy, we theoretically finds that message passing, a key mechanism in graph encoders, trivializes the maximization of positive samples, preventing GCLs from effectively learning from positive samples. To address this, we propose SPGCL to mitigate the trivialization caused by message passing and restore the learning efficacy of positive samples. Specifically, we find that high Dirichlet energy features help positive samples provide effective learning signals while low Dirichlet energy features contribute little to positive learning signal but is useful for positive sampling. Based on this, SPGCL propagates only high Dirichlet energy features and uses low energy features to construct a probability matrix for reliable positive sampling. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of SPGCL.

</details>


### [93] [When Metrics Disagree: A Meta-Analysis of Knowledge-Graph-Completion Model Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10287)
*Haji Gul,Ajaz Ahmad Bhat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将知识图谱补全（KGC）模型评估重构为多准则决策（MCDM）问题，通过元分析七种聚合器在五个测试维度上的表现，发现Z-score是最平衡的聚合器，并揭示了不同测试维度的鲁棒性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGC评估依赖单一排名指标（如MRR、Hits@k、Mean Rank），这些指标在不同数据集上常产生冲突的模型排序，导致比较困难、选择性报告泛滥，且无法反映真实进展。

Method: 将KGC评估形式化为MCDM问题；对七种聚合器进行五项测试（一致性、跨数据集稳定性、指标独立性、噪声鲁棒性、泛化性）；采用留一模型（LOMO）和留一组（LOGO）移除平均策略；进行Pareto最优分析和测试敏感性分析。

Result: Pareto最优分析显示Z-score是最平衡的聚合器；在尾部预测中DualE排名最高，关系预测中FMS排名最高；一致性和稳定性对模型移除不敏感，泛化性和独立性最敏感。

Conclusion: 该框架解决了KGC评估不一致性问题，为聚合器选择和模型基准测试提供了基于证据的指导。

Abstract: Evaluating Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) models remains challenging because standard assessment relies on isolated rank-based metrics such as MRR, Hits$@$k, and Mean Rank, which often produce conflicting model orderings across datasets. A model that leads on MRR may trail on Hits@1, and strong performance on one dataset may not generalize to another. This fragmentation hinders comparison, enables selective reporting, and obscures real progress. We reframe KGC evaluation as a Multi-Criteria Decision-Making (MCDM) problem and present a meta-analysis of seven aggregators across five tests: consistency, cross-dataset stability, metric independence, robustness under noise, and generalizability. Each test is averaged over leave-one-model-out (LOMO) and leave-one-group-out (LOGO) removals so that reliability reflects aggregator behavior across diverse model subsets. Across tail $(h,r,?)$ and relation $(h,?,t)$ prediction, Pareto-optimal analysis identifies Z-score as the most balanced aggregator, which ranks DualE highest for tail prediction and FMS (Flow-Modulated Scoring) highest for relation prediction. A test-sensitivity analysis using the same removals shows that consistency and stability are largely removal-invariant, while generalizability and independence are the most sensitive. The framework resolves evaluation inconsistencies and offers evidence-based guidance for aggregator selection and model benchmarking in KGC.

</details>


### [94] [LLM-Guided Neural Architecture Search for Robust Co-Design of Physical Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10294)
*Tyler King,Timothee Leleu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UH-NAS是一种硬件无关、LLM引导的神经架构搜索框架，通过将LLM作为进化算子，联合优化任务精度与推理能耗，支持跨不同非传统硬件平台（如光学MZI）的公平对比，实验表明其发现的架构比现有方法更多样、更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS方法通常针对单一硬件家族定制，限制了跨平台比较与泛化能力；非传统硬件部署需要同时优化任务精度与硬件特定的能效、物理非理想性、数值精度等约束，亟需一种硬件无关的联合优化框架。

Method: 提出UH-NAS框架，核心思路是将硬件作为可替换的后端（含每平台能耗模型、物理约束和非理想性模拟器），利用LLM作为进化算子驱动搜索过程，在不修改搜索算法的前提下实现跨平台公平比较。

Result: 在光学MZI硬件上，UH-NAS发现的架构比传统基线和现有LLM-to-NAS方法更多样、更鲁棒；消融实验验证了架构在非理想性下的鲁棒性以及系统提示（system prompts）的作用。

Conclusion: 架构与硬件的协同设计对新兴计算平台至关重要，UH-NAS为跨非传统硬件平台的公平NAS比较提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Deploying neural networks on unconventional hardware demands architectures that co-optimize task accuracy and platform-specific constraints such as energy cost, physical non-idealities, and numerical precision. Existing neural architecture search (NAS) methods are typically tailored to a single hardware family, limiting cross-platform comparison and generalization. We introduce Unconventional Hardware Neural Architecture Search (UH-NAS), a hardware-agnostic, LLM-guided NAS framework that integrates language models as evolutionary operators to co-optimize accuracy and inference energy. By exposing hardware as a swappable backend with per-platform energy models, physical constraints, and non-ideality simulators, UH-NAS enables fair system-level comparisons across various backends without modifying the search algorithm. Tested on optical MZI hardware, UH-NAS discovers more diverse, robust architectures than conventional baselines while outperforming existing LLM-to-NAS approaches. Additional ablations on architecture robustness under non-idealities and the role of system prompts highlight the importance of architecture-hardware co-design for emerging computing platforms.

</details>


### [95] [Baseline-Free Policy Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10321)
*Carlos S. Sepúlveda,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将GRPO（组相对策略优化）应用于神经组合优化（NCO）中的路径规划问题。GRPO源于大语言模型对齐技术，通过组内轨迹归一化替代了传统REINFORCE算法中的rollout基线，从而避免了基线脆弱性导致的训练不稳定。实验表明，GRPO在TSP和CVRP任务上均能避免训练崩溃，且无需外部基线即可达到接近POMO的求解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准NCO训练算法REINFORCE使用rollout基线进行方差缩减，但该基线存在结构性脆弱性：在困难实例上，较差的基线会产生噪声梯度估计，导致训练不稳定甚至崩溃。

Method: 采用GRPO算法，该算法通过组内采样轨迹归一化优势函数，完全消除外部基线。在RL4CO框架下，对TSP和CVRP基准测试中的五种RL算法进行受控比较。

Result: (i) GRPO避免了REINFORCE在TSP-100上的训练崩溃（成本从9.8骤升至52.1）；(ii) 在相同梯度更新次数下，GRPO的求解质量与强基线POMO相差在2%以内；(iii) P3O在TSP上表现良好但在CVRP上变异性较高。

Conclusion: GRPO是NCO中一种有前景的无基线替代方案，特别适用于基线依赖训练变得脆弱的场景。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) trains autoregressive policies to solve routing problems. The standard training algorithm, REINFORCE with a rollout baseline, requires maintaining and periodically updating a frozen copy of the policy for variance reduction. This baseline introduces a structural vulnerability: on harder instances, a poor baseline produces noisy gradient estimates that can destabilize training. We evaluate Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), an algorithm from large language model alignment that eliminates the baseline entirely by normalizing advantages within groups of sampled trajectories. In a controlled comparison of five RL algorithms on TSP and CVRP benchmarks within the RL4CO framework, we find that: (i) GRPO avoids the training collapse observed with REINFORCE on TSP-100, where performance degrades from cost 9.8 to 52.1 immediately after the warmup phase and does not recover under extended training; (ii) at matched gradient updates, GRPO achieves solution quality within 2% of POMO, a strong AM-based multi-start baseline, while requiring no external baseline; and (iii) P3O, a pairwise preference algorithm also from the alignment literature, is competitive on TSP but shows higher variability on CVRP. These results identify GRPO as a promising baseline-free alternative for NCO, particularly in settings where baseline-dependent training becomes fragile.

</details>


### [96] [Rank Collapse, Fixed Points, and the Renormalization Group Structure of MLP Residual Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10324)
*Parviz Haggi-Mani,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次量化验证了MLP残差网络的前向传播与重正化群流之间的类比关系：经过训练后，残差流的有效秩随深度单调下降，且这种秩坍缩是选择性的——仅发生在短相关长度的输入序列上，而长相关长度序列则保留全部自由度；层间核漂移集中在少数特定层，其余部分接近不动点，表明网络实现了基于输入分布谱结构的选择性粗粒化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对深度神经网络前向传播与重正化群流之间的类比缺乏定量验证，未定义可测量的RG序参量，也未在控制输入分布变化下进行检验或做出可验证的定量预测。

Method: 在纯MLP残差堆叠架构上训练掩码标记预测任务，使用具有已知谱性质的合成马尔可夫链序列作为输入数据，通过测量残差流的有效秩、跨层核漂移等指标进行定量分析。

Result: (i)训练后残差流有效秩随深度单调下降；(ii)秩坍缩具有选择性：短相关长度(~1)时发生，长相关长度(~7)时消失；(iii)层间核漂移集中在一两层，其余部分接近不动点。

Conclusion: MLP残差网络实现了基于输入分布谱结构的选择性粗粒化过程，为神经网络与重正化群之间的类比提供了首个定量、位置级别的证据。

Abstract: The analogy between deep neural network forward passes and renormalization group (RG) flows has been repeatedly noted in the literature, but existing treatments remain qualitative: depth is described as a coarse-graining scale, attention is likened to a partition function, and representations are said to flow toward fixed points. No existing work has defined a measurable RG order parameter, tested it under controlled variation of the input distribution, or made quantitative predictions that are empirically verified. We study the simplest architecture for which the analogy is tractable: a pure MLP residual stack trained on masked token prediction over synthetic Markov chain sequences with known spectral properties. We report three findings. (i) The effective rank of the residual stream decreases monotonically with depth after training, consistent with progressive integration of irrelevant degrees of freedom. (ii) This rank collapse is selective: it occurs for chains with short correlation length approximately 1 but is absent for chains with long correlation length approximately 7, measured at the position level to control for mean-pooling artifacts. The network preserves exactly the degrees of freedom relevant to the prediction task, the content of the RG relevance criterion. (iii) Inter-layer kernel drift is concentrated at one or two specific transitions, with the remainder of the network near a fixed point, consistent with a discrete fixed-point plateau. Together these findings constitute the first quantitative, position-level evidence that MLP residual networks implement a selective coarse-graining procedure governed by the spectral structure of the input distribution.

</details>


### [97] [Privacy-Preserving Credit Risk Prediction with Alternative Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10333)
*Hongzhe Zhang,Jiarong Xu,Jing He,Xiao Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出隐私保护的信用风险预测问题，并开发PrivacyCredit方法，在保护消费者隐私、保持模型机密性和无损性能的前提下，安全整合替代数据以提升信用风险预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信用风险预测依赖借款人的人口统计、财务和信用历史数据（传统数据），而替代数据（如手机通信数据）能提供更全面的借款人信用画像、提升预测性能；但替代数据由外部实体持有，直接共享会侵犯消费者隐私，现有研究普遍忽视这一问题。

Method: 定义隐私保护信用风险预测问题，同时满足三个约束：隐私保护（保护消费者隐私）、模型机密性（模型由金融机构集中学习和存储）、无损性（维持模型性能）。开发PrivacyCredit方法，一种隐私保护的机器学习方法。

Result: 理论证明了PrivacyCredit的隐私保护、模型机密性和无损性；基于真实信用数据集和替代数据的实验表明，安全整合替代数据具有预测价值，PrivacyCredit实现了与不安全明文组合传统数据和替代数据相同的预测性能，并评估了其模型机密性和计算效率。

Conclusion: PrivacyCredit能够在保护隐私和模型机密性的前提下，无损地整合替代数据以提升信用风险预测性能，为金融行业安全利用外部数据提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Credit risk prediction is a critical problem in the consumer credit industry. Traditionally, financial institutions construct credit risk prediction models using borrowers' demographic, financial, and credit history data, collectively referred to as traditional data. Recent studies have demonstrated that alternative data, such as borrowers' mobile phone communication data, enable lenders to acquire fuller and more accurate profiles of borrowers' creditworthiness, thereby improving credit risk prediction performance. Nevertheless, alternative data are held by external entities independent of financial institutions. Directly sharing alternative data with financial institutions infringe on consumer privacy, yet existing credit risk prediction studies largely overlook this issue. To address this gap, we define a new problem, namely privacy-preserving credit risk prediction with alternative data, which simultaneously considers three practical constraints: the privacy-preserving constraint that protects consumer privacy, the model-confidentiality constraint that learns and stores the model centrally at the financial institution, and the lossless constraint that maintains the performance of the learned model. To solve this problem, we develop PrivacyCredit, a novel privacy-preserving machine learning method. We then theoretically demonstrate the privacy-preserving, model-confidential, and lossless properties of PrivacyCredit. Through extensive experiments using a real-world credit dataset linked with alternative data, we demonstrate the predictive value of securely incorporating alternative data into credit risk prediction and show that PrivacyCredit achieves the same predictive performance as the model learned from the insecure plaintext combination of traditional and alternative data. We further evaluate its model-confidentiality property and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Explaining Predictions: Logic-Based Explanations for Confidence in Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10347)
*Vinícius Peixoto Chagas,Carlos Henrique Leitão Cavalcante,Thiago Alves Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出置信度感知的溯因解释，通过最小置信度阈值(MCT)量化解释的置信度保证，在仅略微增加解释长度的前提下提升最小置信度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统溯因解释仅关注分类行为，可能覆盖低置信度预测的实例，缺乏对预测置信度的解释能力，难以满足高风险决策场景对透明度和可信度的需求。

Method: 引入最小置信度阈值(MCT)概念量化溯因解释的置信度保证；将MCT计算形式化为优化问题；提出生成满足用户指定置信度阈值的最小解释的算法；在二分类提升树模型上验证。

Result: 传统溯因解释提供的置信度保证远弱于被解释实例本身的置信度；置信度感知的解释持续提升解释的最小置信度保证，仅需适度增加解释长度。

Conclusion: 置信度感知的溯因解释适用于需要同时保证预测正确性和置信度的可信决策场景，且可推广到其他提供置信度分数的机器学习模型。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly used in critical domains, where both predictions and their associated confidence levels influence important decisions. To enhance transparency in such scenarios, it is important to understand why a model is confident or uncertain about its predictions. Recent logic-based approaches provide abductive explanations, minimal subsets of features sufficient to preserve the predicted class, with correctness guarantees. However, these methods focus solely on classification behavior and may produce explanations that cover instances with low predictive confidence. In this work, we introduce the concept of Minimum Confidence Threshold (MCT), which quantifies the weakest confidence guarantee provided by an abductive explanation. Building upon this concept, we propose confidence-aware abductive explanations, which preserve not only the predicted class but also a user-specified confidence guarantee. We formulate MCT computation as an optimization problem and introduce an algorithm for generating minimal explanations that satisfy a desired confidence threshold. We evaluate the proposed framework on boosted trees for binary classification, although the approach is applicable to other machine learning models that provide confidence scores. Experimental results show that traditional abductive explanations often provide substantially weaker confidence guarantees than the confidence associated with the explained instance itself. In contrast, confidence-aware explanations consistently improve the minimum confidence guaranteed by an explanation while requiring only a modest increase in explanation length. These properties make the proposed approach particularly suitable for applications where both predictive correctness and confidence are essential for trustworthy decision making.

</details>


### [99] [KG-SoftMAP: Soft Knowledge-Graph Priors for Bayesian Network Structure Learning from Sparse Discrete Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10358)
*Guoliang Xu,James E. Corter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对稀疏离散数据中贝叶斯网络结构学习困难的问题，提出KG-SoftMAP方法，将加权知识图谱编码为可被数据覆盖的软先验，结合BDeu评分与logit先验最大化MAP目标，在合成基准和真实教育数据上展示了优于基线方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏离散数据中学习贝叶斯网络结构极具挑战性：每个实例仅记录少量变量时，大多数变量对缺乏共同观测值，导致评分不可靠，纯数据方法几乎无法恢复任何结构。而领域内通常存在不完美的领域知识，如何有效利用这些知识辅助结构学习是核心动机。

Method: 提出KG-SoftMAP方法，将加权有向知识图谱编码为置信度加权的软边先验（可被数据覆盖），优化结合BDeu评分与logit形式先验的MAP目标函数。知识图谱可来自专家整理或LLM提取。

Result: 在合成基准（唯一有真实DAG的场景）中，KG-SoftMAP在ρ=0.05时恢复部分有向结构（DF1 0.14-0.29，基线接近零），ρ≥0.2时大幅提升（DF1 0.46-0.96），且随KG质量下降性能优雅退化。在真实稀疏教育数据（无真实DAG）上，作为诊断模型在SAF上F1_FAIL比逻辑回归低0.03，但提供KG一致边、校准联合概率和任意子集推理能力。

Conclusion: KG-SoftMAP能有效利用不完美知识图谱从稀疏数据中学习BN结构，性能随KG质量优雅退化。在缺乏有意义KG时，判别式逻辑回归更优；当存在领域知识时，KG-SoftMAP可学得可解释的诊断模型。

Abstract: Learning Bayesian network (BN) structure from sparse discrete data is hard: when each instance records only a few variables, most variable pairs lack the joint observations needed for reliable scoring, and data-only methods recover little structure. Imperfect domain knowledge, expressible as a weighted directed knowledge graph (KG), is often available. We propose KG-SoftMAP, which encodes such a KG as a soft, confidence-weighted, data-overridable edge prior and maximizes a MAP objective combining the BDeu score with a logit-form prior; the KG may be expert-curated or LLM-extracted. On controlled synthetic benchmarks, the only setting with ground-truth DAGs, KG-SoftMAP recovers partial directed structure at $ρ=0.05$ (DF1 $0.14$ to $0.29$, versus near-zero baselines) and substantially more once $ρ\geq0.2$ (DF1 $0.46$ to $0.96$), when paired with an informative but imperfect KG; recovery degrades gracefully as KG quality drops. On real sparse educational data, which has no ground-truth DAG, we evaluate deployment-facing measures only: prediction, calibration, and KG-consistency. The learned BN is best read as a diagnostic model: on SAF it trails logistic regression by $0.03$ F1_FAIL while providing KG-consistent edges, calibrated joint probabilities, and inference from arbitrary observed concept subsets; when no meaningful KG exists, discriminative logistic regression is preferable.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Absolute Imitation: Anchored Residual Guidance for Privileged On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10385)
*Wenhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AR-OPD，通过在特权在线策略蒸馏中引入锚定残差机制，解耦本地可达推理步骤与未来条件先知信号，解决了后见偏差导致的推理捷径问题，在复杂推理任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特权OPD方法将特权信息视为整体模仿目标，未能区分本地可达推理步骤与未来条件先知信号，导致学生模型匹配后见偏差分布，跳过有效中间推理而采用本地不可支持的捷径。

Method: 提出锚定残差在线策略蒸馏（AR-OPD），采用双视图框架：1）使用部分特权教师建立本地兼容锚点；2）将先知预见作为受控残差注入，提供目标导向引导，而非强制执行完整的全局视角模仿。

Result: 在多种推理任务上，AR-OPD比完整特权OPD高出2.3个点，比SFT高出7.9个点；后见泄漏减少21.7%；在超过768 token的长程轨迹上获得高达7.2个点的优势。

Conclusion: AR-OPD通过锚定残差机制有效缓解了后见偏差和后期漂移问题，为特权在线策略蒸馏提供了更优的解耦框架。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has demonstrated strong empirical gains in enhancing complex reasoning in LLMs by aligning a student model with a teacher's predictive distribution over the student's own trajectories. An emerging variant, Privileged OPD, further strengthens this paradigm by employing a self-teacher model augmented with privileged information, such as oracle traces, to mitigate teacher-student capacity gaps while providing dense, answer-directed supervision. However, current methods treat privileged information as a monolithic imitation target, failing to disentangle locally reachable reasoning steps from future-conditioned oracle signals. Consequently, the student is encouraged to match a hindsight-biased distribution that often falls outside its local predictive support. This reachability mismatch incentivizes the student model to skip valid intermediate reasoning in favor of locally unsupported shortcuts. To resolve this, we introduce Anchored Residual On-Policy Distillation (AR-OPD), a dual-view framework that disentangles privileged supervision. Rather than enforcing strict full-view imitation, AR-OPD establishes a locally compatible anchor using a partially privileged teacher, isolating and injecting oracle foresight as a controlled residual to provide destination-directed guidance. Across diverse reasoning tasks, AR-OPD outperforms full privileged OPD by 2.3 points and SFT by 7.9 points. Crucially, this anchored residual mechanism reduces hindsight leakage by 21.7% and mitigates late-stage drift, yielding up to a 7.2-point advantage on challenging long-horizon trajectories exceeding 768 tokens.

</details>


### [101] [Validation-Stage Combinatorial Fusion Analysis for Imbalanced Credit-Card Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10393)
*Xiao Han,Chenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了组合融合分析（CFA）在信用卡欺诈检测中通过选择小规模互补模型子集并分配多样性感知权重，可在IEEE-CIS基准上超越单一强模型（AUC-ROC=0.9405），而CTGAN合成数据增强会劣化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用卡欺诈交易具有罕见、高成本且分布不均的特点，现有梯度提升树模型已表现优异，因此需要验证组合融合分析（CFA）是否能在强基线之上进一步带来显著提升。

Method: 采用无数据泄露的60/20/20划分，基于7个基分类器构建480种融合配置，在验证阶段通过CFA选择互补子集并分配多样性感知权重，最终评估包含软投票、堆叠等对比方法。

Result: 最佳配置（DEF WtScore）在测试集上AUC-ROC=0.9405，AUPRC=0.6699，F1=0.6373；1000次Bootstrap置信区间显示三项指标均显著优于最强单一模型；CFA在AUPRC和F1上优于软投票和堆叠；CTGAN合成数据导致性能下降。

Conclusion: CFA的价值不在于合并所有分类器，而在于验证阶段作为一种选择小规模互补子集并分配多样性感知权重的方法，在欺诈检测场景中具有实际增益。

Abstract: Credit-card fraud detection is difficult because fraudulent transactions are rare, costly, and unevenly distributed. Strong gradient-boosted tree models already perform well on structured transaction data, so the value of another fusion method is not obvious. This paper examines whether Combinatorial Fusion Analysis (CFA), which searches over model subsets and rank-score fusion rules, can still add value on the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection benchmark. Using a leakage-free 60/20/20 train/validation/test protocol, we evaluate 480 fusion configurations built from seven base classifiers. The best test-set result comes from diversity-weighted score fusion of Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM (DEF WtScore), with AUC-ROC = 0.9405, AUPRC = 0.6699, and F1 = 0.6373. Bootstrap confidence intervals from 1,000 resamples show that the gains over the strongest single model exclude zero for all three metrics. CFA matches soft voting on AUC-ROC, improves AUPRC and F1, and outperforms stacking in this setting. A CTGAN augmentation experiment gives a negative result: synthetic fraud samples degrade both individual models and CFA. Overall, CFA is most useful here not as a way to combine every classifier, but as a validation-stage method for choosing a small, complementary subset and assigning diversity-aware weights.

</details>


### [102] [FOGO: Forgetting-aware Orthogonalization Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10406)
*Toan Nguyen,Yang Liu,Trung Le,Celso de Melo,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出遗忘是通用优化现象而非持续学习独有问题，并设计了FOGO优化器，通过谱正交化动量更新和紧凑码本内存解决梯度干扰，在多种任务上优于Adam和Muon。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明遗忘是标准训练中普遍存在的优化现象，而不仅是持续学习的问题；现有优化器无法有效解决梯度干扰导致的知识遗忘问题。

Method: 提出FOGO优化器：(1) 谱正交化动量更新，防止主导方向垄断优化；(2) 基于随机投影的紧凑码本内存存储代表性历史方向，在低维空间保持成对距离；(3) 通过轻量正交校正和近端步骤解决当前更新与存储方向间的冲突，无需存储数据。

Result: 在类别不平衡分类、领域/类别漂移下的持续视觉学习、LLaVA-7B持续微调、GPT-2预训练等任务中，FOGO均持续改进收敛和知识保留，优于Adam和Muon。

Conclusion: 遗忘是通用优化问题，FOGO通过正交化动量更新和码本记忆机制有效缓解梯度干扰，是一种可扩展的通用优化器。

Abstract: We argue that forgetting is not confined to continual learning but is a general optimization phenomenon: during standard training, dominant mini-batch gradients suppress rare but useful update directions, causing short-term forgetting at every step. When such knowledge is never revisited, these losses compound into long-term forgetting-the classical failure mode of continual learning. We introduce FOGO, a scalable optimizer that continuously detects and resolves gradient interference across both regimes. FOGO spectrally orthogonalizes momentum updates to prevent dominant directions from monopolizing optimization, then stores representative past directions in a compact codebook memory built on random projection, where pairwise distances are provably preserved in low-dimensional space. At each step, conflicts between the current update and stored directions are resolved via lightweight orthogonal correction and lifted back through a proximal step, with minimal overhead and no data storage. Across class-imbalanced classification, continual visual learning under domain and class shifts, continual fine-tuning of LLaVA-7B, and GPT-2 pretraining, FOGO consistently improves convergence and knowledge retention, outperforming Adam and Muon.

</details>


### [103] [A Comprehensive Inference-Time Augmentation Framework in Physiological Signals: Application to PPG-Based AF Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10410)
*Davood Fattahi,Runze Yan,Saurabh Kataria,Zhaoliang Chen,Xiao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的推理时增强（ITA）框架，包含13种增强方法和贝叶斯超参数优化，在PPG房颤检测任务中显著提升了模型鲁棒性（AUROC提升最高8.5%，AUPRC提升最高10.6%），是一种无需重新训练即可提升部署可靠性的模型无关方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理信号在真实部署中面临传感器噪声、运动伪影和分布偏移等挑战，而现有的推理时增强（ITA）方法仅局限于少数增强方式和固定未优化的参数，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出统一的ITA框架，包含13种增强方法（覆盖时域、幅域、频域和伪影注入变换），通过贝叶斯优化进行超参数搜索；在GPT-PPG和ResNet两种模型上评估房颤检测性能，使用5个数据集、400+患者、约9800小时PPG记录。

Result: 标准ITA持续提升AUROC（GPT-PPG最高8.5%，ResNet最高0.7%）和AUPRC（GPT-PPG最高10.6%，ResNet最高0.8%）；选择性ITA在非AF数据集上降低平均FPR（GPT-PPG降低4.4%，ResNet降低1.3%）。

Conclusion: ITA是一种实用且模型无关的方法，能在无法重新训练的部署场景中提升PPG房颤分类的可靠性，并对更广泛的生理信号分析具有普适性。

Abstract: Objective: Accurate classification of physiological signals in real-world deployments is challenged by sensor noise, motion artifacts, and distribution shifts between training and deployment data. Inference-time augmentation (ITA), which applies augmentations during inference rather than retraining, offers a simple, model-agnostic mechanism to improve robustness. However, ITA application to physiological signals has remained narrow in scope, relying on limited augmentation methods with fixed, unoptimized parameters. This work proposes a unified ITA framework to address that gap.
  Approach: The framework incorporates 13 augmentation methods spanning time-domain, amplitude-domain, frequency-domain, and artifact-injection transformations, with hyperparameters optimized via Bayesian optimization. We evaluate on atrial fibrillation (AF) detection from 30-second PPG signals using GPT-PPG and ResNet across five datasets comprising more than 400 patients and ${\sim}$9,800 hours of recording.
  Main results: Standard ITA consistently improved AUROC (up to 8.5% for GPT-PPG and 0.7% for ResNet) and AUPRC (up to 10.6% for GPT-PPG and 0.8% for ResNet). Selective ITA further reduced average FPR by up to 4.4% (GPT-PPG) and 1.3% (ResNet) on non-AF datasets.
  Significance: These findings establish ITA as a practical, model-agnostic approach for improving PPG-based AF classification reliability in deployment settings where retraining is not feasible, with broader applicability to physiological signal analysis.

</details>


### [104] [Parallel Causal Associative Fields: Gated Sparse Memory for Long-Context Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10435)
*Muhammad Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并行因果关联场(PCAF)，一种基于并行内容寻址记忆的新型语言建模方法，通过哈希桶存储上下文窗口中的局部记录，为当前查询检索有限候选集，形成稀疏缓存分布并与参数化局部语言模型混合。在WikiText-103和PG-19数据集上，303M参数的PCAF在困惑度和吞吐量上均优于同等规模稠密Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的自注意力机制随上下文长度二次方增长，而RNN和状态空间模型将历史压缩为固定大小的状态，存在信息瓶颈。本文旨在探索第三种范式：一种不依赖二次方注意力也不依赖固定大小状态压缩的并行内容寻址记忆机制。

Method: PCAF通过哈希桶将上下文窗口中的局部记录写入，为当前查询检索有限候选集，形成稀疏缓存分布，并通过可学习门控将该缓存与参数化局部语言模型混合。该方法避免了单一的固定循环状态瓶颈，同时保持稀疏的长上下文访问。

Result: 303M参数、上下文长度T=2048时，PCAF-semantic在WikiText-103上困惑度36.31，PG-19上52.45；匹配稠密Transformer分别为47.49和53.84。PCAF在TPU v4-32 pod上处理速度0.61-0.62M tokens/s，稠密和局部注意力基线为0.43M tokens/s。

Conclusion: PCAF通过关联缓存、检索容量和可学习门控的协同设计，在速度-质量权衡上取得显著优势，验证了并行内容寻址记忆作为Transformer和RNN之外第三种语言建模基元的有效性。

Abstract: Transformers achieve strong language modeling performance by providing direct token-to-token communication paths, but causal self-attention scales quadratically with context length. Recurrent and state-space models reduce this cost, yet compress history into sequentially updated fixed-size states. This paper studies a third primitive: a parallel content-addressed memory over causal successor records. The proposed Parallel Causal Associative Field (PCAF) writes local records from a context window into hash buckets, retrieves a bounded candidate set for the current query, forms a sparse cache distribution over successor tokens, and mixes that cache with a parametric local language model through a learned gate. The resulting model maintains sparse long-context access while avoiding a single fixed recurrent state bottleneck. We evaluate PCAF under full autoregressive pretraining on WikiText-103 and PG-19 using a distributed Google Cloud TPU v4-32 pod. At 303M parameters and context length T = 2048, PCAF-semantic reaches 36.31 perplexity on WikiText-103 and 52.45 perplexity on PG-19, compared with 47.49 and 53.84 for a matched dense Transformer. PCAF-semantic simultaneously processes 0.61-0.62M tokens/s across the TPU pod, versus 0.43M tokens/s for dense and local attention baselines. Supporting 41M-parameter multi-seed sweeps and single-GPU component ablations show that the associative cache, retrieval capacity, and learned gate materially affect the speed-quality trade-off.

</details>


### [105] [SpenseGPT: Practical One-shot Pruning Enabling Sparse and Dense GEMMs for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10445)
*Jaeseong Lee,Seung-won Hwang,Samyam Rajbhandari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Spense提出了一种混合稀疏-稠密格式，将权重矩阵分为2:4稀疏区域和稠密区域，在保持与现有高性能GEMM库兼容的同时放松了稀疏约束。基于此格式的SpenseGPT方法在Qwen3-32B和Seed-OSS-36B模型上实现了B200 GPU上FP8精度下高达1.2x的端到端解码加速，且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的2:4结构化稀疏格式虽然被现代加速器广泛支持并提供2x理论加速，但其严格的50%稀疏约束在训练后剪枝中常导致不可忽略的精度损失。而现有的放松稀疏度格式要么需要专门的编译器支持，要么引入运行时开销限制了端到端加速。

Method: 提出Spense混合稀疏-稠密格式，将每个权重矩阵划分为2:4稀疏区域和稠密区域。基于此格式提出SpenseGPT一次性训练后剪枝方法，并设计了两种选择稠密区域的策略。

Result: 在Qwen3-32B和Seed-OSS-36B模型上，使用B200 GPU和FP8精度实现了高达1.2x的端到端解码加速，同时保持了模型质量。这是首个在B200等现代GPU上通过半结构化稀疏张量核心实现真实端到端LLM解码加速的一次性剪枝演示。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合稀疏-稠密格式有效解决了2:4稀疏的精度损失问题，同时无需自定义编译器支持或输入激活扩展，在保持精度的前提下实现了显著的端到端加速。

Abstract: Semi-structured 2:4 sparsity is widely supported by modern accelerators, providing up to a 2x theoretical speedup. However, its strict 50% sparsity constraint often causes non-negligible accuracy degradation under post-training pruning. Meanwhile, existing relaxed sparsity formats either require specialized compiler support or introduce runtime overheads that limit end-to-end speedup. We propose Spense, a practical hybrid sparse-dense format that splits each weight matrix into a 2:4 sparse region and a dense region. This design relaxes the effective sparsity constraint while remaining compatible with existing high-performance sparse and dense GEMM libraries, avoiding both custom compiler support and input activation expansion. Building on this format, we introduce SpenseGPT, a one-shot post-training pruning method that produces sparse and dense regions. Notably, we show that selecting the right dense regions is important, and we devise two different strategies to choose them. Experiments on Qwen3-32B and Seed-OSS-36B demonstrate that our method achieves up to 1.2x end-to-end decoding speedup on B200 GPUs with FP8 precision, while preserving accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first one-shot pruning demonstration of real-world end-to-end LLM decoding speedup from semi-structured sparse tensor cores on recent GPUs such as B200s, while maintaining model quality.

</details>


### [106] [Mitigating Bias in Low-SNR Financial Reinforcement Learning via Quantum Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10448)
*Zeyu Liu,Xuanzhi Feng,Sing Kwong Lai,Yuanchen Gao,Xiaoyi Pang,Hualei Zhang,Jingcai Guo,Jie Zhang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FPQC-SAC方法，通过在actor和critic网络前插入紧凑有界的参数化量子电路（PQC）来约束特征传播，解决金融市场的低信噪比问题，避免Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）方法中的"金融熵陷阱"。在投资组合管理任务中，累计回报比标准SAC提升66.89%，比最优连续控制深度强化学习基线提升约27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场是低信噪比环境，导致SAC等最大熵离线策略方法不稳定。噪声状态表示产生不可靠的Q值估计，自举（bootstrapping）放大这些误差，形成"金融熵陷阱"失效模式。

Method: 在actor和critic网络前插入紧凑有界的参数化量子电路（PQC）来约束特征传播，而非过滤原始输入或对自举后的Q值进行正则化。利用量子纠缠保持灵活的跨资产交互，通过PQC约束降低极端市场波动对Bellman目标估计的影响。

Result: 在真实投资组合管理任务中，FPQC-SAC累计回报比标准SAC提升66.89%，比最优连续控制深度强化学习基线提升约27%。

Conclusion: FPQC-SAC是一种高效、即插即用的SAC变体，能够有效增强样本外稳定性和累计回报，代码已开源。

Abstract: The financial market is a typical low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) setting, which often destabilizes off-policy maximum-entropy methods like Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). Specifically, noisy state representations may produce unreliable Q-value estimates, and bootstrapping amplifies these errors, forming a failure mode we call the "Financial Entropy Trap". In this paper, we propose FPQC-SAC, an efficient and plug-and-play SAC variant that places a compact and bounded Parameterized Quantum Circuit (PQC) before the actor and critic networks to constrain feature propagation at the representation level, rather than filtering raw inputs or regularizing Q-values after bootstrapping. Notably, FPQC-SAC reduces the impact of extreme market fluctuations on Bellman target estimation, while trainable quantum entanglement preserves flexible cross-asset interactions. Empirical evaluations on real-world portfolio management tasks demonstrate that FPQC-SAC substantially enhances out-of-sample stability and cumulative returns by achieving a 66.89% relative gain in cumulative return over standard unconstrained SAC and outperforms the best continuous-control deep reinforcement learning baseline by approximately 27%. Open-source code is available at https://github.com/ZeyuLIU-UST/FPQC-SAC-main.

</details>


### [107] [ERAlign: Energy-based Representation Alignment of GNNs and LLMs on Text-attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10461)
*Xianlin Zeng,Fan Xia,Xiangyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于能量模型的表示对齐框架（ERAlign），通过能量函数优化GNN和LLM在共享潜空间中的分布一致性，解决文本属性图上跨模态表示对齐问题，在8个数据集上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖启发式粗粒度匹配，缺乏充分约束且忽略分布对齐，导致表示漂移和泛化能力受限。

Method: 构建能量基表示对齐框架，将GNN编码的图结构与LLM生成的文本嵌入投影到共享潜空间，通过距离度量量化层间对齐并用EBM目标优化；引入能量差异（ED）避免高采样成本，同时保证训练效率和能量景观保真度。

Result: 在8个TAG数据集上，在不同监督程度和跨任务迁移场景下均取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: ERAlign通过能量基框架有效实现GNN与LLM的分布一致性对齐，解决了表示漂移问题，且能量差异机制在理论上保证了高效训练。

Abstract: Text-attributed Graphs (TAGs) incorporate textual node attributes with graph structures to describe rich relational semantics. Recent efforts to integrate Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for learning on TAGs, yet achieving well-aligned representations remains challenging. Prior studies largely rely on heuristics that perform coarse-grained matching. They lack sufficient constraints and ignore distributional alignment, leading to representation drift and limited generalization. Building on Energy-based Models (EBMs), we propose an Energy-based Representation Alignment (ERAlign) framework that projects GNN-encoded graph structure and LLM-derived text embeddings in a shared latent space to achieve distribution consistency. Concretely, layer-wise alignment is quantified by a distance metric and optimized via an EBM objective. By decreasing energy values, our framework yields well-aligned representations for downstream tasks. During training, we introduce Energy Discrepancy (ED) to avoid high sampling costs associated with intractable normalization. ED also carries theoretical guarantees of higher training efficiency and reduced energy landscape distortion. Empirical evaluations on eight TAG datasets demonstrate that ERAlign obtains state-of-the-art performance across varying levels of supervision and cross-task transfer scenarios.

</details>


### [108] [UPLOTS: A Unified Pretrained Language Model for Constrained Time-series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10466)
*Du Yin,Hao Xue,Jinliang Deng,Yang Yang,Shuang Ao,Arian Prabowo,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UPLOTS 是一个统一的可提示引导的预训练语言模型框架，用于跨领域的约束时间序列生成，通过动态多数据集损失重加权和提示到模式映射，实现在单一模型下的多样化时间结构生成，并在四个真实基准和多种约束设置下验证了其泛化性和数据增强能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列生成方法通常为每个数据集手工设计或单独训练模型，导致可扩展性差，且无法利用跨领域共享的时间结构。

Method: 提出 UPLOTS 框架，使用单一预训练 Transformer 主干网络，结合可学习的约束提示（constraint prompts），通过动态多数据集损失重加权和提示到模式映射（prompt-to-pattern mapping），在训练时内化多种时间结构并在推理时按需生成。

Result: 在四个真实世界基准和多种约束设置（峰值周期、日历、负载水平、波动模式）下评估；在保留的约束组合和下游预测实验中，UPLOTS 展现出超出原始峰值模式设置的泛化能力，并在数据稀缺场景下改善了数据增强效果。

Conclusion: UPLOTS 通过统一框架和提示引导机制，有效解决了时间序列生成中的碎片化问题，实现了跨领域的泛化生成和有效的数据增强。

Abstract: In time-series generation, existing approaches typically handcraft ortrain a separate model for each dataset, which hinders their scalability and fails to leverage shared temporal structures across domains. To address this fragmentation, we propose UPLOTS, a Unified, Prompt-guided Language model framework fOr constrained Time-Series Generation across diverse domains. Instead of building task-specific models, UPLOTS leverages a single pre-trained transformer backbone guided by learned constraint prompts, enabling on-demand generation with precise pattern control. One key innovation is our dynamic multi-dataset loss re-weighting and prompt-to-pattern mapping, which allows UPLOTS to internalize diverse temporal structures during training and conditionally generate them at inference. We evaluate UPLOTS on four real-world benchmarks and multiple constraint settings, including peak-period, calendar, load-level, and volatility patterns. Additional held-out constraint-combination and downstream forecasting experiments further demonstrate that UPLOTS generalizes beyond the original peak-pattern setting and improves data augmentation under scarce real-data regimes. Our code and baselines are available at anonymous github repo: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UPLOTS-6C36.

</details>


### [109] [Advancing the State-of-the-Art in Empirical Privacy Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10481)
*Nicole Mitchell,Galen Andrew,Arun Ganesh,Brendan McMahan,Peter Kairouz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过高温采样（T≥0.8）从LLM生成合成金丝雀数据，用于参数高效微调中的隐私审计，并引入基于辅助模型的合成数据审计方法，系统研究了模型容量与金丝雀熵对记忆化的交互影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调的LLM存在记忆化训练样本的问题，而经验隐私审计（EPA）面临如何设计混合在隐私敏感训练数据中的"金丝雀"示例这一关键挑战。

Method: 1) 通过高温采样（T≥0.8）从LLM生成合成金丝雀数据，作为高影响力异常值；2) 提出基于微调辅助模型的合成数据审计方法；3) 系统研究模型容量与金丝雀熵对记忆化的交互效应。

Result: 合成金丝雀具有高可识别性，能实现强审计；由于本身非私有，可检查且可重复插入而不危害真实数据隐私；辅助模型审计方法能有效估计合成数据的隐私泄露。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了EPA中金丝雀设计的核心难题，并为理解模型容量与数据熵对记忆化的影响提供了系统性的实证基础。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) can exhibit problematic memorization of individual training examples. Empirical privacy auditing (EPA) quantifies this risk by measuring realistic data leakage on membership inference (MI) or reconstruction attacks. A key challenge in EPA is designing ``canary'' examples that are mixed with the privacy-sensitive training data. We propose generating synthetic canaries via high-temperature sampling ($T \geq 0.8$) from LLMs, using prompts tailored to the privacy-sensitive training data. These canaries act as high-influence outliers, ensuring high identifiability and hence strong audits. Further, since the canaries are themselves non-private, they are inspectable and can be inserted with repetition without jeopardizing the privacy of the real data. An important use of models fine-tuned on privacy-sensitive data is the generation of synthetic data. This also comes with privacy risk. We introduce a powerful synthetic data audit based on fine-tuning an auxiliary model on the synthetic data. Auditing the auxiliary model for the original canaries then provides a strong estimate of the privacy leakage through the synthetic data. Finally, leveraging our strong auditing methodologies, we perform a systematic investigation into the interacting effects of model capacity and canary entropy on memorization.

</details>


### [110] [Stop Early, Spend Less: Hidden-State Probes as a Practical Recipe for Streaming Moderation of LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10487)
*Huizhen Shu,Xuying Li,Piao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG
