Method: 在合成多任务Transformer和DistilGPT-2/GPT-2的LoRA适配器上进行实验，通过分析任务-梯度结构的局部低秩性、验证固定任务平面假设、构建轨迹-前缀基、推导随机搜索的高斯局部线性定理，以及测量参数扰动与激活引导之间的余弦相似度等方法来系统研究线性结构。

Result: 发现了强局部低秩任务-梯度结构但拒绝了固定任务平面假设；轨迹-前缀基可捕获77%的LoRA恢复位移；参数扰动与CAA激活引导向量之间余弦相似度为0.58。

Conclusion: 训练网络中的线性结构并非全局任务方向，而是演化中的局部几何结构，这些结构在参数空间和激活空间之间存在部分持久性。

Abstract: Task vectors, LoRA, activation steering, and random search around pretrained weights all suggest that learned behaviour can be controlled by linear directions. We ask which linear structures actually exist and on what scale. In a synthetic multitask transformer and LoRA adapters on DistilGPT-2 / GPT-2 we find strong local low-rank task-gradient structure but reject the fixed-task-plane hypothesis: static bases miss the recovery direction, and the useful basis drifts substantially within 100 steps. However, the first recovery updates form a trajectory-prefix basis capturing 77% of the LoRA recovery displacement. We develop random search theory with a Gaussian local-linear theorem that justifies the effectiveness of random parameter search even in very high dimensions. We also study the relation between parameter perturbations and activation steering: a single gradient step produces an activation shift with 0.58 cosine to a labelled-contrast CAA steering vector, with a similar steering effect on Qwen-0.5B BoolQ statements. We validate our results with experiments on synthetic Transformers and LLMs. Our results suggest that linear structures in trained networks are not global task directions, but evolving local geometries that partially persist across parameter and activation spaces.

</details>


### [148] [When Do Autoregressive Sequence Models Forecast Physical Wavefields? A Controlled Study on Synthetic Seismograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10868)
*Waleed Esmail,Stuart Russell,Jana Klinge,Alexander Kappes,Christine Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过合成三分量地震图作为测试平台，系统研究了自回归预测模型在长时间滚动预测中的稳定性问题，发现多token预测是稳定性的主导因素，而相位漂移和极性反转是主要失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域自回归预测振荡物理信号（如地震图、引力波应变等）时，因误差累积导致相位漂移，而逐点指标无法检测此类问题。本文旨在探究滚动预测保持稳定的条件。

Method: 以SeismoGPT自回归预测器为模型，在合成三分量地震图上进行受控的架构内消融实验，通过自由运行滚动预测结合配对显著性检验，隔离各设计选择（多token预测、混合预测头、跨视窗STFT幅度相干损失）的贡献。

Result: 多token预测是主导稳定因素，较单token基线提升中位NCC 0.040；地平线嵌入混合预测头和STFT幅度相干损失带来小幅但一致的改进；性能在上下文比率阈值接近1时急剧下降，该阈值大致等于观测信号的完整P-S间隔；主要残余失效模式为极性反转。

Conclusion: 本文不是架构基准测试，而是对振荡波场滚动稳定性的受控研究。相位感知的目标函数是解决极性反转问题的自然下一步方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoregressive forecasting of oscillatory physical signals, such as seismograms, gravitational-wave strain, and similar wavefields is limited by error accumulation: as a causal model is fed its own outputs over hundreds of steps, small per-step errors compound into phase drift that pointwise metrics fail to detect. We ask when such rollout stays stable, using synthetic three-component seismograms as a physically structured testbed and the \textsc{SeismoGPT} autoregressive forecaster as the model under study. Through controlled, intra-architecture ablations evaluated on free-running rollout with paired significance tests, we isolate the contribution of each design choice. Multi-token prediction is the dominant stabilizer, accounting for almost the entire improvement over a single-token baseline ($+0.040$ median NCC); a horizon-embedding hybrid prediction head and a cross-horizon STFT-magnitude coherence loss each add a small but consistent further gain. Performance depends sharply on a context-ratio threshold near one, roughly the full P-S interval of observed signal, below which rollout generalization collapses. The dominant residual failure is a polarity inversion that a magnitude-based spectral loss cannot, by construction, penalize, identifying phase-aware objectives as the natural next step. We frame this as a controlled study of rollout stability on oscillatory wavefields, not a benchmark of forecasting architectures.

</details>


### [149] [CLP: Collocation-Length Prediction for Zero-Loss Adaptive Multi-Token Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10935)
*Xuezhen Xie,Zhiqiang Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Backbone-as-Architect设计原则和CLP（搭配长度预测器），通过让主干语言模型头始终生成第一个token，并引入轻量级单线性层（4.6K-7.7K参数）预测可安全接受的额外token数量，解决了多token预测中主干头与MTP头的竞争问题，在Qwen2.5模型上实现了1.14x-1.29x加速且零质量退化，而传统门控网络方法加速效果差且产生严重退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTP加速方法存在根本性架构缺陷：MTP第一个token的头与主干LM头竞争，导致预测被接受时出现严重的质量退化（重复和不连贯输出）。作者识别出这种"头-主干竞争"是先前MTP加速方法中重复和不连贯输出的根本原因。

Method: 提出Backbone-as-Architect设计原则（主干LM头始终生成第一个token，MTP头只负责后续token）和CLP（Collocation-Length Predictor），一个轻量级span级决策层，仅用单线性层（4.6K-7.7K参数）预测每个解码步可安全接受的额外token数量，替代了以往1M参数的过工程化门控网络。

Result: 在Qwen2.5（0.5B、1.5B、7B）上，CLP在1.5B上实现1.20x-1.29x加速，在7B上实现1.14x-1.20x加速，重复率<0.02（零质量退化）；而门控方法仅1.07x加速且重复率>0.5%。更短的预测视野（k=2）在大模型上恢复24%的MTP头准确率。

Conclusion: 识别出MTP头预测准确率是加速的约束瓶颈，建立了清晰的改进路线图。更短预测视野在大模型上更有效，形成了可扩展感知的设计原则。

Abstract: Large language model inference is bottlenecked by autoregressive decoding, where each token requires a full forward pass. Multi-token prediction (MTP) offers a promising acceleration path, but existing approaches suffer from a fundamental architectural flaw: the MTP head for the first token competes with the backbone's own language model (LM) head, leading to severe quality degradation when predictions are accepted. We identify this head-backbone competition as the root cause of repetitive and incoherent outputs in prior MTP-based acceleration methods. To address this, we propose Backbone-as-Architect, a design principle where the backbone LM head always generates the first token, and MTP heads are responsible only for subsequent tokens. Building on this principle, we introduce CLP (Collocation-Length Predictor), a lightweight span-level decision layer that predicts how many additional tokens can be safely accepted at each decoding step. CLP uses only a single linear layer (4.6K--7.7K parameters), replacing the over-engineered 1M-parameter gate networks used in prior work. Experiments on Qwen2.5 models (0.5B, 1.5B, 7B) show that CLP achieves 1.20x--1.29x speedup on 1.5B and 1.14x--1.20x on 7B, with zero quality degradation (repetition ratio < 0.02), while gate-based approaches fail to accelerate (1.07x) or produce severely degraded outputs (repetition ratio > 0.5%). We further demonstrate that shorter prediction horizons (k=2) recover 24% higher MTP head accuracy on large models, establishing a scaling-aware design principle. We identify MTP head prediction accuracy as the binding constraint on acceleration and establish a clear roadmap for future improvements.

</details>


### [150] [XtrAIn: Training-Guided Occlusion for Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10877)
*Thodoris Lymperopoulos,Ioannis Kakogeorgiou,Denia Kanellopoulou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出XtrAIn方法，通过将遮挡操作从输入空间转移到参数空间，沿模型训练轨迹测量特征相关参数更新对输出logits的影响，解决了遮挡归因方法中基线选择偏差、分布外样本和归因偏移等问题，在图像数据集和乳腺癌分类任务上生成了更清晰、更可解释的归因模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遮挡归因方法存在三个主要问题：1）外部选择的基线会引入偏差和分布外样本；2）非线性模型中遮挡某些特征会导致未遮挡特征的归因分数发生漂移（即归因偏移）；3）解释结果不稳定。

Method: XtrAIn将遮挡操作从输入空间转移到参数空间，沿模型训练轨迹测量特征相关参数更新对输出logits的影响，避免了人工基线。同时提出Xstep（轻量级近似降低计算成本）和XtrAIn+（强调与目标类别对齐的更新）两种变体。

Result: 在受控图像数据集和PAM50乳腺癌亚型分类任务上，XtrAIn及其变体比标准归因基线生成了更清晰、更可解释的归因模式。

Conclusion: XtrAIn提供了一种训练感知的特征归因新视角，可作为研究训练过程中特征级证据形成的诊断工具。

Abstract: Occlusion-based attribution methods provide an intuitive way to estimate feature importance by perturbing input features and measuring the resulting change in model output. However, their reliability is strongly affected by how feature removal is implemented: externally selected baselines can introduce bias, out-of-distribution samples, and unstable explanations, while in nonlinear models the occlusion of a set of features can also alter the contribution of non-occluded features. We refer to this effect as attribution shift, as the attribution scores of the non-occluded features drift from their initial values. To challenge these major issues that render explanations unstable, we introduce XtrAIn, a training-guided attribution method that transfers the occlusion operation from the input space to the parameter space. Instead of replacing input values with hand-crafted baselines, XtrAIn follows the model's training trajectory and measures how feature-associated parameter updates affect the output logits. We further introduce Xstep, a lightweight approximation for reducing computational cost, and XtrAIn+, a target-focused variant that emphasizes updates aligned with the target class. Experiments on controlled image datasets and PAM50 breast-cancer subtype classification show that the proposed methods produce cleaner and more interpretable attribution patterns than standard attribution baselines. Overall, XtrAIn provides a training-aware perspective on feature attribution and offers a useful diagnostic tool for studying how feature-level evidence is formed during training.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond Uniform Token-Level Trust Region in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10968)
*Renjie Mao,Xiangxin Zhou,Lvfang Tao,Yixin Ding,Yu Shi,Yongguang Lin,Yuheng Wu,Honglin Zhu,Qian Qiu,Wenxi Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PPO-style RLVR中位置无关的均匀阈值问题，提出CPPO（累积前缀散度策略优化），通过位置加权阈值和累积前缀预算两种机制对齐自回归生成特性，显著提升训练稳定性和推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPO-style信任区域机制对所有token施加统一的散度阈值，忽略了自回归生成中两个关键问题：(1) 自回归不对称性——早期token的偏差会导致序列级累积漂移，均匀阈值对早期约束不足而对后期过度约束；(2) 忽略前缀累积漂移——相同token级偏差在不同历史偏离程度下影响不同。需要一种能感知位置和累积前缀偏差的约束机制。

Method: 提出CPPO，包含两个耦合机制：(1) 位置加权阈值——对早期位置施加更严格限制，对后期token放松约束；(2) 累积前缀预算——追踪历史偏差，动态限制后续token的偏差，防止前缀上的累积错误。

Result: 实验表明CPPO在不同模型规模上均能提升训练稳定性和推理准确率。

Conclusion: CPPO通过感知位置和累积前缀的散度约束机制，有效解决了RLVR中均匀阈值与自回归生成特性之间的冲突，是一种更优的信任区域更新方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become standard for improving LLM reasoning. However, existing PPO-style trust-region mechanisms remain position-agnostic by enforcing uniform thresholds across all tokens independently. This pointwise treatment conflicts with autoregressive generation in two critical ways. First, uniform thresholds ignore autoregressive asymmetry. Early-stage deviations produce compounding sequence-level drift, causing static thresholds to under-regulate early divergence and excessively constrain late-stage exploration. Second, evaluating token-level divergence in isolation overlooks cumulative prefix drift, granting the same divergence allowance regardless of how far the conditioning history has already deviated from the rollout policy. To address this limitation, we propose CPPO (Cumulative Prefix-divergence Policy Optimization), a token-level masking rule that aligns updates with a finite-horizon policy-improvement bound via two coupled mechanisms. First, a position-weighted threshold imposes stricter limits at early positions whose effects persist longer, relaxing constraints for late-stage tokens. Second, a cumulative prefix budget tracks historical deviations, dynamically restricting further token-level deviation to prevent compounding errors along the prefix. Empirically, CPPO enhances training stability and significantly improves reasoning accuracy across various model scales.

</details>


### [152] [AuRA: Internalizing Audio Understanding into LLMs as LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11033)
*Bo Cheng,Lei Shi,Zhanyu Ma,Yuan Wu,Jun Xu,Jiuchong Gao,Jinghua Hao,Renqing He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuRA 通过层间蒸馏将语音编码能力注入大语言模型，在轻量级 LoRA 适配下实现高效的端到端语音-语言联合建模，无需级联流水线或大规模多模态训练，在多个基准上超越级联系统、语音-LLM 适配基线及大规模多模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将大语言模型扩展到语音输入的方法（级联 ASR-LLM 流水线、端到端语音语言模型、桥接/蒸馏适配）存在转录接口延迟、昂贵的多模态训练或串行语音-语言耦合等问题，亟需一种更高效、轻量且可复用预训练组件的语音-语言联合建模方法。

Method: AuRA 将同一语音输入同时送入 ASR 编码器（教师）和 LoRA 适配的 LLM（学生），通过轻量级音频嵌入层注入语音特征，并采用逐层蒸馏对齐学生的隐藏状态与教师的对应表示，从而将语音表示内化到 LLM 侧的轻量适配中，支持并行的端到端推理。

Result: 在多个语音-语言基准上，AuRA 在效果和效率上一致优于级联系统、语音-LLM 适配基线以及大规模语音-语言和多模态模型。

Conclusion: AuRA 证明了通过蒸馏将语音编码能力注入 LLM 是一种有效的轻量级替代方案，同时实现紧密的语音-语言联合建模与高效并行推理，且无需大规模多模态训练。

Abstract: Recent efforts to extend large language models (LLMs) to speech inputs typically rely on cascaded ASR-LLM pipelines, end-to-end speech-language models, or bridge/distillation-based adaptation. While these routes respectively reuse strong pretrained components, enable native speech-language interaction, or offer lightweight adaptation, they often suffer from transcript-interface latency, costly multimodal training, or sequential speech-language coupling. To address these limitations, we present AuRA, a method that distills audio encoding capability into the LLM. Specifically, AuRA feeds the same speech input to an ASR encoder (as a teacher) and a LoRA-adapted LLM (as a student) through a lightweight audio embedding layer, and uses layer-wise distillation to align the student's hidden states with corresponding teacher representations, thereby internalizing speech representations into lightweight LLM-side adaptations. Compared with cascaded and serial bridge methods, AuRA enables tighter speech-language joint modeling and efficient parallel end-to-end inference, while also reusing pretrained speech and language models rather than requiring large-scale multimodal training. On multiple speech-language benchmarks, AuRA consistently outperforms cascaded systems, speech-to-LLM adaptation baselines, and large-scale speech-language and multimodal models in both effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [153] [Flash-GMM: A Memory-Efficient Kernel for Scalable Soft Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10896)
*Gal Bloch,Ariel Gera,Matan Orbach,Ohad Eytan,Assaf Toledo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flash-GMM是一个融合的Triton内核，能够在单次GPU pass中高效计算大规模高斯混合模型（GMM），通过避免在GPU内存中实例化完整的责任矩阵，相比现有实现实现20倍加速，并支持在单个设备上训练比之前大100倍以上的数据集。将其集成到IVF粗量化器中用于近似最近邻搜索，软GMM聚类可作为k-means的替代方案，达到固定召回目标时减少1.7倍距离计算，或在同等计算成本下提高2-12个recall@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GMM实现需要将完整的责任矩阵（n个数据点×k个高斯分量）实例化在GPU内存中，导致内存瓶颈限制了可处理的数据规模，且计算效率低下。

Method: 设计了一个融合的Triton内核，在单次GPU pass中同时计算期望步（E-step）和最大化步（M-step），避免中间责任矩阵的显式存储；将Flash-GMM作为IVF粗量化器的软聚类替代k-means硬聚类，并利用责任矩阵将边界向量分配到多个聚类中。

Result: 1) 与现有实现相比获得20倍加速；2) 支持训练比之前大100倍以上的数据集；3) 在ANN搜索中达到固定召回目标时减少1.7倍距离计算，或在同等计算成本下提高2-12个recall@10。

Conclusion: 软GMM聚类可作为k-means的可行替代方案用于ANN搜索的粗量化器，且所提出的融合内核实现了高效的内存利用和计算性能。

Abstract: We present \textbf{Flash-GMM}, a fused Triton kernel for efficient computation of Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) over large-scale data in a single GPU pass. By eliminating the need to materialize the full responsibility matrix in GPU memory, Flash-GMM achieves a \textbf{20$\times$} speedup over existing implementations and enables training on datasets more than \textbf{100$\times$} larger than previously feasible on one device. To demonstrate its impact, we integrate Flash-GMM into the IVF coarse quantizer for approximate nearest-neighbor (ANN) search. We show that soft GMM clustering is now a viable drop-in replacement for $k$-means, and that GMM responsibilities can be leveraged to assign border vectors to multiple clusters. Our approach reaches fixed recall targets with up to $1.7\times$ fewer distance computations, or equivalently, yields $+2$--$12$ recall@10 at matched computational cost. We release the kernel as an open-source project.

</details>


### [154] [Unifying Local Communications and Local Updates for LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11081)
*Pietro Cagnasso,Eugene Belilovsky,Edouard Oyallon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GASLoC是一种新型去中心化预训练算法，通过将通信加速推广到"外部优化器"，实现了兼容自适应优化器、支持局部优化步骤和稀疏随机对等通信的高效去中心化训练框架，在异构带宽场景下显著优于DiLoCo等现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模语言模型预训练依赖同步All-Reduce操作，这在带宽异构或工作节点速度不一致时成为瓶颈。现有去中心化方法在LLM场景下性能有限，亟需一种既能减少通信频率又能处理异构性的高效训练算法。

Method: GASLoC将通信加速概念推广到"外部优化器"(outer optimizer)，构建基于八卦(gossip)协议的去中心化训练框架，支持自适应优化器(如Adam)、局部优化步骤以及稀疏随机化对等通信。

Result: 在标准LLM训练任务中，GASLoC在单步每通信设置下优于最先进的去中心化算法；在使用多局部步骤时，性能与DiLoCo相当；在异构带宽场景下显著优于DiLoCo。

Conclusion: GASLoC为去中心化LLM预训练提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案，尤其在异构环境中具有明显优势，兼容现代优化器并支持灵活的训练配置。

Abstract: Communication-efficient pre-training of LLMs is increasingly important as training draws on compute distributed across clusters, data centers, and lower-bandwidth links. Many practical methods reduce communication frequency but still rely on synchronous All-Reduce operations that maintain identical model states and tie progress to global collectives. This can become a bottleneck when bandwidth or worker speed is heterogeneous. We introduce GASLoC, a novel decentralized pre-training algorithm that generalizes the notion of communication acceleration to the recently popular "outer optimizer" to allow a practical gossip-based training framework that is compatible with adaptive optimizers, allows for local optimizer steps, and can utilize sparse randomized peer communication. Empirically, on a number of standard LLM training tasks, we demonstrate that GASLoC outperforms state-of-the-art decentralized algorithms in single step per communication setting for a number of topologies and, unlike existing decentralized methods in the LLM setting, it allows to obtain performance competitive with DiLoCo when utilizing multiple local steps. In the heterogeneous bandwidth setting we demonstrate the advantage of GASLoC showing that it can significantly outperform DiLoCo.

</details>


### [155] [Conservation Laws from Data Symmetry in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10913)
*Jakob Galley,Vahid Shahverdi,Axel Flinth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了训练数据的固有对称性是否会在神经网络的梯度流训练中导致守恒量。证明在解析非多项式损失函数下，数据对称性通常不产生额外积分；但在MSE损失下，数据增强可能产生额外守恒量，并通过"可张量化网络"框架解释这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索训练数据的对称性是否会在神经网络梯度流训练中产生守恒量（即不变量），理解数据增强对训练动力学的影响。

Method: 理论分析：证明在解析非多项式损失函数下，数据对称性一般不导致额外守恒量；针对MSE损失特殊情况，提出"可张量化网络"框架（包括线性网络、多项式网络、Lightning Attention等）来描述数据增强带来的额外守恒量。

Result: 解析非多项式损失下数据对称性不产生额外守恒量；MSE损失下数据增强可产生额外守恒量，该现象可通过可张量化网络框架解释。

Conclusion: 数据对称性是否导致守恒量依赖于损失函数类型和网络架构，MSE损失下的可张量化网络存在额外守恒量，而非多项式损失下则不具备该性质。

Abstract: We explore whether intrinsic symmetries of the training data lead to conserved quantities during gradient-flow training of neural networks. Under the assumption that the loss function is analytic and non-polynomial, we prove that data symmetries generically do not induce any additional integrals of motion. For mean squared error (MSE) loss, on the other hand, there are situations in which data augmentation yields extra conserved quantities. We build a framework, utilizing \emph{tensorizable networks} to describe this phenomenon. Tensorizable networks are a family of architectures whose dependence on parameters and inputs can be separated using an intermediate representation. They include linear and polynomial networks, as well as Lightning Attention.

</details>


### [156] [Test-Time Gradient Guidance of Flow Policies in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11087)
*Zhiyuan Zhou,Andy Peng,Charles Xu,Qiyang Li,Tobias Springenberg,Kevin Frans,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QGF算法，通过预训练参考流策略和值函数，在测试时利用值梯度引导策略生成高价值动作，无需额外策略学习即可实现强化学习策略优化，在离线RL基准上表现出色且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流模型等表达能力强的连续控制策略在监督模仿学习中表现优异，但将其整合到强化学习（RL）流程中进行策略改进时面临困难，通常需要专门的训练目标或通过去噪过程反向传播，导致稳定性问题和可扩展性受限。本文研究是否可以在测试时单独进行简单的策略改进，同时保持稳定的监督策略训练，以此作为回避上述问题的竞争性替代方案。

Method: 提出QGF算法：预训练一个参考流策略（通过标准行为克隆目标）和一个值函数评论家；在测试时，使用值梯度引导参考策略生成更高价值的动作，完全无需额外的策略学习。

Result: 在具有高维动作空间的单任务和目标条件离线RL基准上，QGF优于以往的测试时RL方法；与最先进的训练时算法相比具有竞争力，同时运行成本更低；通过避免演员-评论家训练的不稳定性，展现出随模型规模扩展的良好特性。

Conclusion: 测试时策略优化可以成为训练时RL算法的实用且有效的替代方案，结合表达能力强的策略模型，在保持稳定性的同时实现竞争性能。

Abstract: Expressive continuous control policies, such as diffusion and flow models, form the backbone of recent advances in scaling imitation learning for simulated and real robot control. While they are known to scale stably in the supervised imitation learning setting, incorporating them into reinforcement learning (RL) pipelines for policy improvement has proven more difficult. It often requires specialized training objectives or backpropagating through denoising processes, which cause well-known issues with stability and affect scalability. In this paper we study the question of whether simple policy improvement schemes at test time alone, leaving stable supervised policy training intact, can be a competitive alternative which sidesteps these issues. To this end, we propose QGF (Q-Guided Flow), an RL algorithm that performs policy optimization entirely at test time. QGF works by pre-training both a reference flow policy (via a standard behavioral cloning objective) and a value function critic and, at test time, using the value gradient to guide the reference policy to generate higher-value actions without any additional policy learning. Empirically, QGF outperforms prior test-time RL methods on single-task and goal-conditioned offline RL benchmarks with high-dimensional action spaces, and is competitive with state-of-the-art training-time algorithms while being much cheaper to run. Moreover, it exhibits favorable scaling with model size by avoiding the instability of actor-critic training, offering a practical and effective alternative RL algorithm with expressive policies.

</details>


### [157] [TRACE: A Unified Rollout Budget Allocation Framework for Efficient Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11119)
*Heming Zou,Qi Wang,Yun Qu,Yuhang Jiang,Lizhou Cai,Yixiu Mao,Ru Peng,Xin Xu,Weijie Liu,Kai Yang,Saiyong Yang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多轮智能体强化学习中奖励信号对比度不足的问题，本文提出TRACE框架，通过将ReAct式回合建模为语义节点并在固定采样预算内自适应分配预算到提示根和中间前缀节点，构建树状展开结构，从而增强奖励对比度，提升策略更新信号。在典型智能体基准上取得了竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在多轮智能体场景中面临奖励对比度不足的问题：过于简单或复杂的提示产生低方差反馈，且仅使用结局奖励时，多轮展开中每个决策被赋予相同的终端评估。以往的预算分配方法仅考虑提示层面的样本信息量，忽略了同一轮展开中不同回合前缀层面的信息量差异。

Method: 将每个ReAct风格的思考-行动-观察回合建模为语义上独立的节点，使预算分配从提示根扩展到回合级别的前缀节点及其后续展开，形成树状结构展开。TRACE框架通过一个通用的可泛化预测器，根据前缀历史估计条件成功概率，从而将展开预算分配到最可能产生混合终端奖励的提示根和中间前缀节点上。

Result: 在典型智能体基准上取得竞争性表现和效率提升，例如在使用相同采样成本的情况下，Qwen3-14B在多跳问答上的平均准确率提升了2.8个点。

Conclusion: TRACE通过树状展开预算分配有效增强了奖励对比度，在固定采样预算下提升了多轮智能体RLVR的训练效率和性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a promising approach for enhancing reasoning and agentic behavior in large language models. However, rollout-intensive policy optimization is often limited by insufficient reward contrast, arising when overly simple or complex prompts generate low-variance feedback and when outcome-only rewards assign the same terminal assessment to every decision in a multi-turn rollout. Past efforts have focused on allocating available rollout resources to promising prompts, yet they only leverage sample informativeness at the prompt level and neglect variation in prefix-level informativeness across turns within the same rollout. This work targets multi-turn agentic RL by modeling each ReAct-style thought-action-observation turn as a semantically distinct node, allowing budget allocation to extend from prompt roots to turn-level prefixes with further continuations, which naturally forms tree-structured rollouts. We introduce Tree Rollout Allocation for Contrastive Exploration (TRACE), a unified rollout allocation framework that enhances reward contrast within a fixed sampling budget. Technically, TRACE allocates rollout budget to both prompt roots and intermediate prefixes that are most likely to yield mixed terminal rewards. A shared generalizable predictor estimates conditional success probability at these anchors from prefix histories to guide this allocation. The resulting adaptive tree structure enriches outcome-only feedback and amplifies the policy-update signal. Empirically, TRACE achieves competitive performance and efficiency gains on typical agentic benchmarks, e.g., improving Qwen3-14B Multi-Hop QA average accuracy by 2.8 points over competitive baselines at equal sampling cost.

</details>


### [158] [A Systematic Approach for Selecting Trajectories for Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10938)
*Adam Nordling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性评估了五种轨迹数据增强选择策略（Outlierness、Diversity、Representativeness、Uncertainty和Random），发现Outlierness和Uncertainty策略在稀疏数据集上表现更稳定，但增强效果具有条件依赖性：对稀疏数据有效修复拓扑碎片，对高质量密集数据反而引入噪声，并在高速度域存在物理局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹数据增强方法（如几何扰动）依赖随机选择，缺乏对"哪些轨迹应被增强"的系统性理解，导致增强效益有限。本文旨在填补这一空白，开发一个系统化、可扩展的框架来评估不同选择策略。

Method: 在四个数据集（动物行为Foxes/Starkey、海上交通AIS、城市交通Car）上，使用五种选择策略（Outlierness、Diversity、Representativeness、Uncertainty、Random）结合线性/非线性机器学习模型进行测试，并集成Optuna超参数优化循环来经验性地确定最佳增强参数。

Result: 系统性策略并非通用解决方案，但Outlierness和Uncertainty策略比随机基线更稳定，在密集数据集中不易出现性能退化。UMAP可视化显示系统性增强可修复稀疏数据集的拓扑碎片，但在高质量密集数据集中会成为噪声信号。高速度域中标准扰动技术会导致特征空间发散。

Conclusion: 轨迹数据增强的价值具有严格的条件依赖性：系统性选择策略在稀疏数据场景下优势明显，但在密集、高质量数据或高速度域中可能适得其反。增强策略的选择应根据数据特性谨慎决定。

Abstract: Trajectory data augmentation is a promising approach to mitigate data scarcity in machine learning applications, but its utility has been limited by the complexity of preserving spatio-temporal coherence. Although prior work demonstrated the viability of geometric perturbation, it relied on naive random selection, leaving a critical gap in understanding which trajectories should be augmented for maximal benefit. This thesis addresses this gap by developing a systematic and scalable framework to evaluate five systematic selection strategies: Outlierness, Diversity, Representativeness, Uncertainty, and Random selection. These strategies were rigorously tested across four datasets covering animal behavior (Foxes and Starkey), maritime traffic (AIS), and urban traffic (Car) using a suite of linear and non-linear machine learning models. As part of this evaluation, an Optuna-based hyperparameter optimization loop was integrated to empirically identify the best-performing augmentation parameters for each dataset within the explored search space. The results indicate that, while systematic selection is not a universal solution, it offers distinct advantages over the random baseline. Systematic strategies, particularly Outlierness and Uncertainty, demonstrated higher stability and were less prone to performance degradation observed with random sampling in dense datasets. However, the findings also reveal that the value of augmentation is strictly conditional. Visual analysis via UMAP demonstrates that while systematic augmentation successfully repairs topological fragmentation in sparse datasets, it can act as a corrupting noise signal in high-quality, dense datasets. Furthermore, the study identified physical limitations in high-velocity domains, where standard perturbation techniques lead to divergence in feature space...

</details>


### [159] [EEVEE: Towards Test-time Prompt Learning in the Real World for Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11182)
*Weixian Xu,Shilong Liu,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EEVEE，首个面向LLM智能体的多数据集测试时提示学习框架，通过路由-提示协同进化策略实现异构任务流下的鲁棒学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时提示学习方法仅针对单数据集设计，无法处理真实应用中多数据集、多领域、多任务分布组成的异构输入流，存在严重的跨数据集干扰问题，限制了实际部署能力。

Method: 引入路由器将输入划分为任务聚类并分配至合适提示配置；采用路由-提示协同进化策略，交替执行路由学习和提示学习以解决二者的相互依赖问题。

Result: 在多个数据集上，EEVEE比Qwen3-4B-Instruct和DeepSeek-V3.2分别提升10.38和24.32个平均多基准分，超越SOTA方法GEPA和ACE达37.2%和48.2%。

Conclusion: EEVEE在保持单基准学习能力的同时，显著提升了异构数据流下的鲁棒性和效率，为LLM智能体在真实多任务场景中的测试时学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose EEVEE, the first multi-dataset test-time prompt learning framework for LLM agents, enabling test-time prompt learning under real-world task streams. Existing methods are largely designed for single-dataset settings, while real-world applications require models to handle heterogeneous input streams drawn from multiple datasets, domains, and task distributions, limiting their practical applicability. To mitigate cross-dataset interference, EEVEE introduces a router that partitions incoming inputs into task clusters and assigns them to suitable prompt configurations. This design is optimized via a router-prompt co-evolution strategy, which employs interleaved router and prompt learning phases to address their mutual dependency. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that the framework improves robustness under heterogeneous data streams while maintaining single-benchmark learning capability and efficiency. Specifically, EEVEE improves average multi-benchmark scores by 10.38 and 24.32 points over Qwen3-4B-Instruct and DeepSeek-V3.2, surpassing SOTA methods GEPA and ACE by up to 37.2% and 48.2%.

</details>


### [160] [Express Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10944)
*Albert Gong,Annabelle Michael Carrell,Raaz Dwivedi,Lester Mackey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Express的新工具，能将非因果注意力近似转换为因果注意力近似，并保持相同近似保证。结合最先进的Thinformer近似，Express在因果注意力上实现了目前最优的$\log^{3/2}(n)/s$近似误差，仅需$O(s)$内存和$O(s^2 \log^2(n))$压缩开销。配合I/O感知的Triton实现，在速度上显著超越FlashAttention 2，并在语言建模pipeline中克服了四个资源瓶颈：长上下文预填充、KV缓存压缩、长形式内存受限解码和长形式计算受限解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力近似方法在处理因果注意力（causal attention）时存在近似保证不足或资源开销过大的问题，亟需一种既能保持理论保证又能高效实现因果注意力近似的工具。

Method: 设计Express工具将非因果注意力近似转换为因果注意力近似，结合Thinformer近似实现理论保证；采用I/O感知的Triton实现以优化实际运行效率。

Result: 在因果注意力上实现了$\log^{3/2}(n)/s$近似误差，仅需$O(s)$内存和$O(s^2 \log^2(n))$压缩开销；实际速度显著超越FlashAttention 2；在四个语言建模瓶颈任务上均取得性能提升。

Conclusion: Express提供了一种兼具理论保证和实际效率的因果注意力近似方案，能有效缓解长序列语言建模中多个资源瓶颈问题。

Abstract: We introduce a new tool, Express, for converting a non-causal attention approximation into a causal approximation with matching approximation guarantees. When combined with the state-of-the-art Thinformer approximation, Express improves upon the best known causal attention guarantees, delivering $\log^{3/2}(n)/s$ approximation error with only $O(s)$ memory and $O(s^2 \log^2(n))$ compression overhead for a sequence of length $n$. We pair these developments with an efficient I/O-aware Triton implementation, demonstrate substantial speedups over FlashAttention 2, and use Express to overcome four resource bottlenecks in the language modeling pipeline: long-context prefill, KV cache compression, long-form memory-constrained decoding, and long-form compute-constrained decoding.

</details>


### [161] [A Unifying Lens on Supervised Fine-Tuning Through Target Distribution Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11189)
*Tong Xie,Yuanhao Ban,Yunqi Hong,Sohyun An,Yihang Chen,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视监督微调（SFT），提出将其视为目标分布设计问题，引入Q-target框架分解SFT监督为两个显式选择，并基于此提出Target-SFT方法，在10个推理数据集-模型设置中取得一致优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SFT最大化轨迹中每个token的似然，但观测token可能非唯一、带噪声或与模型先验不一致，严格拟合独热目标在模型具有丰富先验知识时是次优的。

Method: 提出Q-target框架，将SFT监督分解为：(1)对观测token的依赖强度，(2)剩余概率质量在备选token上的分配方式。基于此提出Target-SFT，直接从期望的目标分布构建训练目标。

Result: 在10个推理数据集-模型设置上，Target-SFT一致优于现有SFT变体。

Conclusion: 该公式揭示了SFT训练更根本的设计原则，开辟了SFT目标函数的更广阔搜索空间。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) typically maximizes the likelihood of every token in a demonstrated trajectory. However, an observed token can be non-unique, noisy, or misaligned with the model prior. Strictly fitting toward this one-hot target may be suboptimal, especially when the pretrained model encodes a rich knowledge prior. In this work, we reinterpret SFT as target distribution design: instead of studying only the loss objective, we analyze the token-level target that the loss drives the model to match. We introduce the Q-target framework, which decomposes SFT supervision into two explicit choices: (1) how strongly to rely on the observed token, and (2) how to allocate the remaining probability mass over alternatives. This perspective unifies many existing SFT variants as implicit choices of the target distribution Q. Building on this view, we propose Target-SFT which constructs the training objective directly from the desired target distribution. This method consistently outperforms across the ten reasoning dataset-model settings evaluated, showing the effectiveness of this target-based approach. Overall, our formulation reveals a more fundamental design principle for SFT training and opens a broader search space for SFT objectives.

</details>


### [162] [Population-Aware Physics-Informed Neural Particle Flow for Bayesian Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10959)
*Batu Candan,Simone Servadio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PA-PINPF，通过引入置换不变的Deep Sets表示来编码粒子群体信息，从而改进物理信息神经粒子流（PINPF）方法。实验表明，基于特征群体的编码方式效果最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINPF的粒子速度模型独立处理每个粒子，未显式利用粒子群体的经验分布信息，限制了贝叶斯后验传输的精度。

Method: 在PINPF框架中引入置换不变的Deep Sets表示，将完整的粒子集合信息编码到每个粒子的更新中。设计了两种编码器：PA-PINPF-State（摘要粒子状态）和PA-PINPF-Feature（摘要局部物理信息特征向量，包括位置、伪时间、测量信息、似然值和分数信息）。

Result: 在测距任务和非线性到达时间差后验传输任务上，两种群体感知变体均优于逐粒子的PINPF，其中基于特征群体的编码（PA-PINPF-Feature）表现最强。

Conclusion: 群体层面的物理特征能够为学习的贝叶斯粒子传输提供有用的全局信息，提升后验近似的质量。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural particle flow (PINPF) learns a deterministic transport field that moves particles from a prior distribution toward a Bayesian posterior while enforcing the governing probability-evolution equation. However, the standard PINPF velocity model processes particles independently and therefore does not explicitly condition its transport decisions on the empirical particle population. This paper introduces population-aware PINPF (PA-PINPF), which augments each particle update with a permutation-invariant Deep Sets representation of the full particle set. We investigate two population encoders. PA-PINPF-State summarizes the particle states, whereas PA-PINPF-Feature summarizes the complete local physics-informed feature vectors, including particle position, pseudo-time, measurement information, likelihood values, and score information. The latter allows the population context to represent not only particle-cloud geometry, but also the population-level Bayesian transport geometry. The methods retain the original unsupervised physics-informed residual objective and require no ground-truth posterior samples during training. Experiments on range-measurement tasks and nonlinear time-difference-of-arrival posterior transport demonstrate that both population-aware variants improve over particle-wise PINPF, while feature-population encoding provides the strongest performance. These results show that population-level physics features provide useful global information for learned Bayesian particle transport.

</details>


### [163] [Learning Doubly Sparse Explicitly Conditioned Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10975)
*Tudor Pistol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构化的、显式条件化的可学习变换，将其表示为固定标准矩阵与数据自适应稀疏分量的乘积，在保留快速稳定分析变换优势的同时引入可控的数据适应性。算法基于不精确近端方法框架，利用新推导的闭式投影算子，在双稀疏变换学习问题上达到SOTA，且计算成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解析变换（如DFT、DCT）虽高效且鲁棒，但假设数据具有固定先验，无法准确捕捉更受限信号类别的特定结构；而数据自适应学习变换虽能降低残差项，但缺乏结构约束。本文旨在结合二者的优势，提出一种兼具泛化能力和低近似误差的变换学习方法。

Method: 将目标变换建模为固定标准矩阵（如DCT）与一个可学习的稀疏精炼分量的乘积。利用条件数作为度量指标，在不精确近端方法框架下推导了闭式投影算子，实现高效优化。

Result: 在双稀疏变换学习问题上达到SOTA结果；与稠密变体性能相当，但计算成本显著更低，有时收敛更快且能更好地避免局部最小值。

Conclusion: 该结构化解耦方法有效保留了分析变换的快速性与稳定性，同时引入了可控的数据自适应性，为变换学习提供了一种新颖且高效的范式。

Abstract: Finding convenient spaces in which certain hypotheses regarding an assumed sparse structure of natural signals hold true has become a desirable result in recent research, its implications being reflected in areas such as data compression, noise reduction and feature extraction. While the extensively used analytical transforms, such as DFT or DCT, already provide efficient algorithms and robust sparse representations, they assume a fixed prior about the data, failing to accurately capture the specific structure of more restrictive classes of signals. To address this, the concept of a data-adaptive, learnt transform has been introduced in the literature, allowing for the reduction of a residual term in the transform domain. More recent studies have shown that the condition number serves as a good metric in this context, where the desired outcome alternates between a generalizing tendency and one that achieves minimal approximation error. Motivated by these considerations, we introduce the learning of a structured, explicitly conditioned transform formulated as the product of a fixed canonical matrix and a refining data-adaptive sparse component. This approach seeks to preserve the advantages of fast and stable analytical transforms, while introducing controllable adaptivity to the data. No references that concern this specific formulation have been identified so far, indicating its novelty. The proposed algorithm is motivated within the framework of inexact proximal methods, leveraging a newly derived closed-form projection operator. Empirical observations demonstrate state-of-the-art results on the doubly sparse transform learning problem and comparable performance with its dense variant at significantly lower computational costs and sometimes faster convergence and better avoidance of bad local minima.

</details>


### [164] [Data-Driven Runway and Taxiway Exits Prediction of Landing Aircraft: A Case Study at Hartsfield-Jackson Atlanta International Airport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11017)
*Alex Porcayo,Yutian Pang,Maria Thomas,John-Paul Clarke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对亚特兰大机场滑入阶段的决策问题，提出了一种两阶段数据驱动决策辅助框架。第一阶段预测进港飞机选择的跑道出口，第二阶段预测飞机会穿越活动起飞跑道还是使用端绕滑行道。实验表明XGBoost和LightGBM表现最优，进近速度是出口选择的关键特征，少数类预测仍具挑战性。该框架提供可解释、校准的预测以支持管制员态势感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机场地面运行日益成为高吞吐量枢纽的性能瓶颈，需要数据驱动的方法辅助管制员决策，提升滑入阶段运行效率和安全性。

Method: 提出两阶段预测框架：Stage I预测跑道出口选择，Stage II预测穿越活动跑道还是使用端绕滑行道。使用ASDE-X轨迹数据、飞机特征、机位目的地、短时交通流量和天气数据，训练Random Forest、XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost等九种分类器，并通过t-SNE和UMAP进行特征空间可视化分析。

Result: Stage I准确率0.86-0.89，macro-F1为0.40-0.50；Stage II准确率0.70-0.74，macro-F1为0.28-0.55。XGBoost和LightGBM优于Random Forest。进近速度是出口选择的主要驱动因素；离港率、穿越率、机位目的地和所选出口是穿越与端绕路径的最强预测因子。少数类因特征空间重叠而预测困难。

Conclusion: 该框架通过可校准、可解释的预测增强管制员态势感知，同时将最终路径决策权保留给人类，兼顾自动化辅助与人为责任。

Abstract: Airport surface operations increasingly constrain performance at high-throughput hubs. This study examines arrival taxi-in decisions at Hartsfield-Jackson Atlanta International Airport (KATL) and proposes a two-stage, data-driven decision aid that mirrors controller workflow. Stage I predicts the runway exit selected by an arriving aircraft. Stage II predicts whether, given that exit, the aircraft will cross the active departure runway at a designated point or use the end-around taxiway. Models are trained using ASDE-X surface trajectories, aircraft characteristics, ramp destinations, short-horizon traffic rates, and weather across multiple look-back windows. We benchmark nine classifiers, including Random Forest, XGBoost, LightGBM, and CatBoost, and evaluate accuracy, macro-F1, precision-recall behavior, confusion matrices, Brier score, and Expected Calibration Error. Across east and west flows, XGBoost and LightGBM outperform Random Forest. Stage I achieves 0.86-0.89 accuracy with macro-F1 scores of 0.40-0.50, while Stage II achieves 0.70-0.74 accuracy with macro-F1 scores of 0.28-0.55. Feature-importance analysis shows that approach speed is the main driver of exit choice. Departure rate, crossing rate, ramp destination, and, for west flow, the selected exit are the strongest predictors of crossing versus end-around routing. Minority classes remain harder to predict because of feature-space overlap, as shown by t-SNE and UMAP analyses. The proposed framework supports controller situational awareness through calibrated, explainable predictions while preserving human responsibility for final routing decisions.

</details>


### [165] [Flow-DPPO: Divergence Proximal Policy Optimization for Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11025)
*Bowen Ping,Xiangxin Zhou,Penghui Qi,Minnan Luo,Liefeng Bo,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow-DPPO，用精确计算的KL散度近端约束替代传统PPO中不适用于流匹配模型的比率裁剪，在图像和视频生成任务中取得更优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将在线RL应用于流匹配模型的方法（如Flow-GRPO、CPS）使用PPO风格的比率裁剪来约束信任区域，但比率裁剪对流模型结构不适配：新旧策略的概率比率是噪声较大的单样本估计，会导致轨迹某些区域过度约束而另一些区域约束不足。

Method: Flow-DPPO提出用散度近端约束替代比率裁剪。核心洞察是流模型中每步策略为高斯分布，可精确且廉价地计算新旧策略间的KL散度。采用非对称散度掩码，仅当更新同时偏离信任区域且违反散度阈值时才阻止梯度更新。

Result: 实验表明Flow-DPPO能获得更高奖励、更好的KL近端效率，缓解灾难性遗忘，促进均衡的多目标优化，并支持稳定的多轮训练（而比率裁剪会退化）。

Conclusion: Flow-DPPO通过散度近端约束有效解决了比率裁剪在流模型中的固有问题，实现了更稳定高效的在线RL微调，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Recent work has demonstrated that online reinforcement learning (RL) can substantially improve the quality and alignment of flow matching models for image and video generation. Methods such as Flow-GRPO and CPS cast the denoising process as a Markov Decision Process and apply PPO-style ratio clipping to enforce a trust region. However, we argue that ratio clipping is structurally ill-suited for flow models: the probability ratio between new and old policies is a noisy, single-sample estimate of the true policy divergence, leading to over-constraining in some regions of the trajectory and under-constraining in others. We propose Flow-DPPO (Flow Divergence Proximal Policy Optimization), which replaces ratio clipping with a divergence proximal constraint. A key observation is that the per-step policy in flow models is Gaussian, enabling exact and cheap computation of the KL divergence between old and new policies. Flow-DPPO employs an asymmetric divergence mask that blocks gradient updates only when they simultaneously move away from the trusted region and violate the divergence threshold. Experiments show that Flow-DPPO achieves higher rewards with better KL-proximal efficiency, alleviates catastrophic forgetting, promotes balanced multi-objective optimization, and enables stable multi-epoch training where ratio clipping degrades. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Tencent-Hunyuan/UniRL/tree/main/FlowDPPO.

</details>


### [166] [Flexible Kernels for Protein Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11057)
*Martin Jankowiak,Yerdos Ordabayev,Rudraksh Tuwani,Henry N. Ward,Hunter Nisonoff,James M. McFarland,Gevorg Grigoryan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用进化替代矩阵和局部线性性质的序列核函数，构建高斯过程模型，在稀疏实验数据下高效预测蛋白质结合亲和力和热稳定性等性质，并通过学习结构感知替代矩阵整合基础模型的结构信息，在多任务学习中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质性质预测（如结合亲和力、热稳定性）在蛋白质设计中至关重要，但稀疏实验数据下的预测仍然面临巨大挑战。

Method: 提出一类序列核函数，利用进化替代矩阵和局部线性性质构建高斯过程模型；通过学习结构感知替代矩阵，整合基础模型的结构信息，适用于多任务学习。

Result: 在数据效率方面优于依赖基础模型嵌入的替代方法；结构条件核在多任务学习中显著优于局部监督学习方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的序列核函数家族为蛋白质性质景观提供了数据高效的建模方法，且能有效整合结构信息用于多任务学习。

Abstract: Despite its importance to applications in protein design, predicting protein properties like binding affinity and thermostability from sparse experimental data remains a significant challenge. Accordingly, we introduce a class of sequence kernels that exploit evolutionary substitution matrices as well as local linearity and demonstrate that the resulting Gaussian processes provide data-efficient models of protein property landscapes, frequently outperforming alternatives that rely on foundation model embeddings. Furthermore--by learning what are in effect structure-aware substitution matrices--we show that our kernels can readily incorporate structural information from foundation models. We demonstrate that these structure-conditioned kernels are well suited to multi-task learning across multiple protein property landscapes and can decisively outperform local supervised learning methods.

</details>


### [167] [GRAFT: Gain-Recalibrated Adapters for Transformer-Based Neural Population Activity Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11066)
*Xiangsheng Ge,Yang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAFT是一个基于Transformer的神经群体活动模型，通过将可重用的时间动态与可重新校准的神经元接口分离，实现了跨天的高效重校准。在NLB'21 MC Maze上达到0.3866 co-bps（集成）的新SOTA，跨天协议下仅更新9.21%参数即可达到0.3749/0.3112/0.3152 co-bps。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经群体活动模型的时间动态与固定神经元集强耦合，限制了其在长期脑机接口中的跨天复用，因为记录神经元的身份、数量和响应统计量会逐天变化。

Method: 提出GRAFT框架，采用Transformer架构，将模型分为可重用的时间动态骨干网络和可重新校准的神经元接口两部分。神经元接口控制记录神经元的加入与离开，并通过辅助增益和位置机制支持Transformer内部的神经活动建模。

Result: 在NLB'21标准协议下，GRAFT集成为0.3866 co-bps，刷新SOTA。在跨天协议下，仅更新9.21%参数即可从MC Maze重校准到Large/Medium/Small数据集，分别达到0.3749、0.3112和0.3152 co-bps。

Conclusion: 接口-骨干分离的设计同时支持了强Transformer神经群体活动建模和数据高效的跨天重校准，为长期BCI应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Neural population activity models can recover rich temporal structure from binned spikes, but their read-in and readout layers often remain tied to a fixed set of recorded neurons. This coupling limits reuse in long-term brain-computer interfaces, where recorded neuron identities, counts, and response statistics can change across days. We introduce GRAFT, a Transformer-based neural population activity model that separates reusable temporal dynamics from a recalibratable neuron interface. The neuron interface controls how recorded neurons enter and leave the shared backbone, and auxiliary gain and positional mechanisms support neural activity modeling inside the Transformer. On MC Maze under the standard NLB'21 protocol, GRAFT reaches 0.3866 co-bps as an ensemble, setting a new state of the art on the primary co-bps metric among public and reported NLB'21 results. In a cross-day protocol constructed from the NLB'21 MC Maze dataset series, GRAFT recalibrates from MC Maze to the scaled MC Maze datasets (Large/Medium/Small) by updating only 9.21% of parameters, reaching 0.3749, 0.3112, and 0.3152 co-bps with restricted target-day support sets. These results show that the same interface-backbone separation supports both strong Transformer-based neural population activity modeling and data-efficient cross-day recalibration.

</details>


### [168] [Exploring the Design Space of Reward Backpropagation for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11075)
*Ruoyu Wang,Boye Niu,Xiangxin Zhou,Yushi Huang,Tongliang Liu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowBP提出一个统一的代理轨迹框架来优化文本到图像流匹配模型，通过将反向传播轨迹本身作为设计对象，解决了直接奖励反向传播中的内存爆炸和梯度链式爆炸问题，包含三个变体（稀疏、桥接、拉格朗日），在多个模型和指标上优于直接梯度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接奖励反向传播在文本到图像流匹配模型中存在两个问题：(1) 完整采样轨迹的激活值无法在现代模型规模下全部存储；(2) 跨步骤的链式雅可比乘积导致早期时间步的奖励梯度膨胀。现有Connector方法（如LeapAlign）虽通过短路径缓解了问题，但梯度质量取决于短路径对完整rollout的近似精度，尤其在长间隔情况下存在不足。

Method: FlowBP构建了一个统一的代理轨迹框架，关键设计包括：(1) 使用无梯度的缓存rollout用于采样；(2) 从缓存和选择性重新前向计算的velocity中构建轻量级反向代理；(3) 框架解耦了四个设计维度：奖励模型输入、激活集、积分权重和桥接耦合，并统一了先前的直接梯度方法。基于此框架实例化了三个变体：FlowBP-Sparse（稀疏欧拉重建）、FlowBP-Bridge（受控桥接耦合）、FlowBP-Lagrange（高阶跳跃求积）。三者均通过激活集大小限制内存，并将梯度链限制在至多一个雅可比因子。

Result: 在SD3.5-M、FLUX.1-dev和FLUX.2-Klein-base三个模型上，基于偏好、质量和组合性指标评估，FlowBP的三个变体在大多数指标上优于直接梯度基线方法。

Conclusion: FlowBP通过将反向轨迹本身作为设计对象，实现了样本高效且可扩展的奖励反向传播优化，在保持内存可控和避免梯度膨胀的同时提升了生成质量。

Abstract: Aligning text-to-image flow matching models with human preferences via direct reward backpropagation is sample-efficient but hampered by two well-known pathologies: activations cannot be stored across the full sampling trajectory at modern model scale, and chained Jacobian products across steps inflate the reward gradient as it travels back to early indices. Connector-based methods, such as LeapAlign, address these issues by replacing the full backward trajectory with a short pinned path, highlighting a useful decoupling between sampling and optimization. However, the quality of the resulting gradient depends on how accurately this short path approximates the full rollout, especially over long intervals. We propose FlowBP, a unified surrogate-trajectory framework that treats the backward trajectory itself as the design object. FlowBP keeps a no-gradient cached rollout for sampling, then builds a lightweight backward surrogate from cached and selectively re-forwarded velocities. This view separates four choices: the reward-model input, active set, integration weights, and bridge coupling, and recovers prior direct-gradient methods as particular settings. Within this framework, we instantiate three variants: FlowBP-Sparse uses sparse Euler reconstruction, FlowBP-Bridge adds controlled bridge coupling, and FlowBP-Lagrange raises the order of leap quadrature. All three bound memory by the active-set size and limit gradient chaining to at most one Jacobian factor. Across SD3.5-M, FLUX.1-dev, and FLUX.2-Klein-base on preference, quality, and compositional metrics, the three variants improve over direct-gradient baselines on most metrics.

</details>


### [169] [Limitations of Learning Tanh Neural Networks with Finite Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11104)
*Philipp Grohs,Matěj Trödler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限精度计算下tanh神经网络的学习局限性，发现基于m个样本的自适应随机算法在L^p范数下收敛率不会超过蒙特卡洛率O(m^{-1/p})，除非采样预算随网络参数和架构规模指数增长。该结果揭示了有限精度对包含局部化尖峰函数的函数类可学习性的根本限制，并将ReLU网络的相关结果推广到了tanh激活函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有限精度计算条件下，使用tanh激活函数的神经网络从点评估中学习的基本局限性，将之前针对ReLU网络的相关结果推广到tanh设定。

Method: 通过迭代tanh激活函数构建高度局部化的尖峰函数（bump functions），以此为基础分析有限精度条件下自适应随机算法的收敛率下界。

Result: 在有限精度设定下，基于m个样本的自适应随机算法在L^p范数中的收敛率无法超过蒙特卡洛率O(m^{-1/p})，除非采样预算随网络参数和架构规模指数增长。

Conclusion: 有限精度对包含局部化尖峰函数的函数类的可学习性施加了根本性限制，该结论从ReLU网络推广至tanh网络。

Abstract: We investigate limitations of learning $\tanh$ neural networks from point evaluations under finite-precision computations and $L^p$ accuracy guarantees, building on Berner, Grohs, and Voigtländer (2023). Our approach is based on a novel construction of sharply localized bump functions via iterated $\tanh$ activations. Using this mechanism, we show that, in a finite-precision setting, no adaptive randomized algorithm based on $m$ samples can achieve a convergence rate higher than the Monte Carlo rate $O(m^{-1/p})$ in the $L^p$ norm, unless the sampling budget grows exponentially with the size of the network parameters and architecture. The results reveal fundamental limitations imposed by finite precision on the learnability of classes containing localized bump functions, extending previous results for ReLU networks to the $\tanh$ setting.

</details>


### [170] [Data-Driven Dynamic Assortment in Online Platforms: Learning about Two Sides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11118)
*Rahul Roy,Nur Sunar,Jayashankar M. Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了双边服务平台中双方选择参数均未知的动态分类问题，提出了一种数据驱动算法，其最坏遗憾呈多对数增长，并证明该算法在速率上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务平台需要在不了解顾客和卖家双方选择模型参数的情况下，动态选择卖家分类以优化平台目标，现有研究尚未同时考虑双边信息不完全的情况。

Method: 构建离散时间模型，顾客依MNL模型从分类中选一卖家发起交易，卖家在固定周期后依另一MNL模型选择一顾客；提出数据驱动算法同时学习双方参数并优化分类决策，采用遗憾指标评估性能。

Result: 算法的最坏遗憾随时间呈多对数增长，并推导出匹配的下界，证明算法在速率上达到最优。

Conclusion: 该文提出的算法在双边信息不完全的动态分类问题中实现了速率最优的遗憾上界，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Abstract: We study a dynamic assortment problem on a two-sided service platform with incomplete information and heterogeneous customers in a discrete-time setting. In each period, a customer arrives seeking service, and the platform chooses an assortment of sellers to display. The customer then proposes a transaction to at most one seller in the assortment according to a multinomial logit choice model. After a fixed number of periods, sellers review the proposals they have received and each chooses at most one customer according to another multinomial logit choice model, after which the cycle repeats. A key challenge is that the platform does not know the choice-model parameters of either customers or sellers in advance. To our knowledge, this is the first study of a dynamic assortment problem in which both sides' choice parameters are unknown. We develop a data-driven algorithm that learns these parameters while optimizing the platform's objective over time. We evaluate performance using regret, which measures revenue loss relative to a clairvoyant benchmark that knows all parameters and customer arrivals in advance. We show that the algorithm's worst-case regret grows polylogarithmically over time, and we derive a matching lower bound, establishing its rate optimality.

</details>


### [171] [Overcoming Rank Collapse in Feedback Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11123)
*Gauthier Boeshertz,Razvan Pascanu,Claudia Clopath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了反馈对齐（FA）在深度架构中扩展性差的原因，发现FA误差的有效秩显著低于BP，限制了参数空间的探索。通过使用Muon优化器和隐藏活动归一化来增加有效维度，在更大架构上显著提升了FA的性能，例如在CIFAR100/ResNet-18上准确率提升9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播（BP）因要求反馈权重为前向权重的转置而被认为生物上不可信。固定随机反馈权重的反馈对齐（FA）虽能规避此问题，但在深层架构中扩展性差，其根本原因尚不明确。

Method: 1) 比较BP与FA模型在CIFAR10上的有效秩差异，发现FA误差的有效秩显著更低；2) 提出两种提升FA有效维度的方法：Muon优化器（正交化权重更新）和隐藏活动归一化（促进激活正交性）。

Result: 在更大架构和基准上，两种方法持续优于FA基线。例如在CIFAR100/ResNet-18上，准确率提升9个百分点。

Conclusion: 低维梯度动态是FA扩展性的关键障碍，诱导更高维度的更新几何是扩展BP替代方案的有前景方向。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) is widely viewed as biologically implausible, in part because it requires feedback weights to be the transpose of forward weights for error propagation. Interestingly, when training a network with fixed random feedback weights to circumvent this issue, learning aligns the forward weights with the feedback weights, leading the backpropagated error signal to become an approximation of the standard gradient used by BP. This process, called Feedback Alignment (FA), occurs in MLPs and very shallow CNNs but does not scale well to deeper architectures. In this work, we first investigated differences between BP and FA models, trained on CIFAR10, specifically focusing on the effective rank of the signal. We found that the FA error has a considerably lower rank and hence is constrained to a lower-dimensional subspace compared to BP, limiting exploration of the parameter space. Motivated by this observation, we evaluated two mechanisms for increasing the effective dimensionality of FA: Muon, an optimiser that orthogonalises weight updates; and hidden activity normalisation, which promotes activation orthogonality. Across larger architectures and benchmarks, we find that these methods consistently improve over FA baselines, for example, on CIFAR100 with a Resnet-18, accuracy increases by 9 percentage points. Our results identify low-dimensional gradient dynamics as a key obstacle to scaling FA and suggest that inducing higher-dimensional update geometry is a promising route toward scaling alternatives to backpropagation.

</details>


### [172] [Robust Regression of General ReLUs with Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11130)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Daniel M. Kane,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在交互式（主动学习）设置下，使用少量标签查询高效学习一般ReLU函数的问题，给出了首个计算高效的算法，并证明了查询复杂度的近最优性以及查询访问的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在被动学习设置下，已有工作给出了使用poly(d,1/ε)个标注样本的高效学习算法。本文旨在探索交互式（查询）设置下能否进一步降低标签复杂度，以及查询访问是否是改善标签复杂度的必要条件。

Method: 提出一个计算高效的学习器，使用d polylog(1/ε)+Õ(min{1/p, 1/ε})个黑盒标签查询，其中p为目标函数的偏置。算法在平方损失下达到O(opt)+ε的误差。

Result: 1) 首个计算高效的交互式学习算法，查询复杂度为d polylog(1/ε)+Õ(min{1/p, 1/ε})；2) 证明了该查询复杂度在忽略计算约束下是定性近最优的；3) 建立了主动学习中标签复杂度的下界：除非抽取超多项式个无标签样本，否则任何主动学习器需要Ω̃(d/ε)个标签。

Conclusion: 在交互式设置下可以显著降低学习一般ReLU所需的标签数量，且查询访问是必要的——若没有查询能力，被动学习无法突破其标签复杂度下界。

Abstract: We study the task of agnostically learning general (as opposed to homogeneous) ReLUs under the Gaussian distribution with respect to the squared loss. In the passive learning setting, recent work gave a computationally efficient algorithm that uses $poly(d,1/ε)$ labeled examples and outputs a hypothesis with error $O(opt)+ε$, where $opt$ is the squared loss of the best fit ReLU. Here we focus on the interactive setting, where the learner has some form of query access to the labels of unlabeled examples. Our main result is the first computationally efficient learner that uses $d polylog(1/ε)+\tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$ black-box label queries, where $p$ is the bias of the target function, and achieves error $O(opt)+ε$. We complement our algorithmic result by showing that its query complexity bound is qualitatively near-optimal, even ignoring computational constraints. Finally, we establish that query access is essentially necessary to improve on the label complexity of passive learning. Specifically, for pool-based active learning, any active learner requires $\tildeΩ(d/ε)$ labels, unless it draws a super-polynomial number of unlabeled examples.

</details>


### [173] [First-Order Trajectory Matching: Fast Ensemble Predictions of Chaotic, Turbulent, Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11138)
*Shreya Jha,Timo Schorlepp,Nicholas Geissler,Jules Berman,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一阶轨迹匹配（FTM）方法，通过学习随机系统轨迹的一阶局部概率质量传输，直接估计概率流速度，避免漂移、扩散和分数估计，实现轨迹感知的集成预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要估计漂移、扩散或分数函数，计算成本高且难以捕捉轨迹级物理量（如通量、环流、势垒穿越流），因此需要一种直接从轨迹学习概率流速度的模拟无关方法。

Method: 通过匹配轨迹的对称一阶运动来学习概率流速度；采用单步模拟无关的损失函数；进行稳定性分析以平衡离散化误差与采样方差。

Result: 在随机动力系统和PDE示例中，FTM能以低确定性滚动成本提供轨迹感知的集成预测；稳定性分析表明离散化误差与采样方差在适当平衡时损失稳定。

Conclusion: FTM是一种有效的替代建模方法，可直接从轨迹学习概率流速度，捕捉轨迹级物理量，兼具计算效率和预测准确性。

Abstract: We introduce First-Order Trajectory Matching (FTM), a surrogate-modeling method that learns the first-order local transport of probability mass from trajectories of stochastic systems. By matching the symmetric first-order motion of trajectories, FTM learns the probability current velocity, whose flow preserves time marginals to match ensemble averages, while also capturing current-like trajectory quantities such as fluxes, circulations, and barrier-crossing currents. FTM learns the current velocity directly from trajectories, avoiding drift, diffusion, and score estimation. Our stability analysis separates discretization error from sampling variance and shows that the one-step simulation-free FTM loss is stable when temporal resolution and sample size are properly balanced. Across stochastic dynamical systems and PDE examples, we empirically demonstrate that FTM provides trajectory-aware ensemble predictions at low, deterministic-rollout cost.

</details>


### [174] [OncoTraj: a public benchmark for longitudinal resistance prediction in EGFR-mutant non-small-cell lung cancer on osimertinib](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11144)
*Abhijoy Sarkar,Aarchi Singh Thakur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OncoTraj是一个包含813名EGFR突变NSCLC患者（一线奥希替尼治疗）的公开基准测试，定义了三个预测任务。研究发现，基于单时间点组织NGS数据的模型在所有任务上均无法超越随机水平，表明当前局限在于输入模态（单次组织活检）而非算法，并提出了未来需引入连续ctDNA数据的设计需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管EGFR突变NSCLC对一线奥希替尼的耐药是治疗压力下克隆演化的经典案例，但目前缺乏用于训练或评估计算模型处理相应纵向患者轨迹的公开基准。

Method: 从三个真实世界临床-基因组数据源（MSK-CHORD 672例、AACR Project GENIE BPC NSCLC 34例、FLAURA分子耐药补充107例）整合了813名患者数据，定义了三个锁定任务：12个月里程碑进展二分类、进展时间回归、耐药机制六分类。提供了数据集、无泄漏保证的数据划分、评估框架及六种基线模型。

Result: 所有模型在单一时间点快照特征上的表现均未超过随机水平，表明当前天花板限制在于输入模态（单次组织NGS）而非算法。但基准确实恢复了可重复的文献一致关联：TP53共突变使12个月进展率从29%提升至59%。

Conclusion: OncoTraj建立了可重复、经泄漏审计的基线，并将模态限制转化为对引入连续ctDNA数据的v2版本的具体设计要求。

Abstract: Resistance to first-line osimertinib in EGFR-mutant non-small-cell lung cancer (NSCLC) is the canonical example of predictable clonal evolution under therapeutic pressure, yet no public benchmark exists for training or evaluating computational models on the corresponding longitudinal patient trajectories. We introduce OncoTraj, a public benchmark of 813 EGFR-mutant NSCLC patients receiving first-line osimertinib, harmonized from three real-world clinical-genomic sources: MSK-CHORD (672 patients), AACR Project GENIE BPC NSCLC (34 patients), and the FLAURA molecular-resistance supplement (107 patients). OncoTraj defines three locked tasks: (A) binary classification of progression by a fixed 12-month landmark, (B) regression of time-to-first-progression in days, and (C) six-class classification of the dominant resistance mechanism. We release the harmonized dataset, patient-level train/validation/test splits with an audited no-leakage guarantee, an open-source evaluation harness, and six reference baselines spanning a majority-class predictor, logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, an LSTM, and a multi-task transformer. With v1's single-timepoint snapshot features, no task clears chance on clean within-source evaluation: the uniformity of this ceiling across every model class localizes the limit to the input modality (single-snapshot tissue NGS rather than serial ctDNA), not the algorithm. The benchmark does recover a reproducible literature-consistent association: TP53 co-mutation raises the 12-month progression rate from 29% to 59% cohort-wide. OncoTraj establishes a reproducible, leakage-audited baseline and converts the modality limit into concrete design requirements for a serial-ctDNA-enriched v2.

</details>


### [175] [Efficiently Learning Drifting Halfspaces with Massart Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11149)
*Mingchen Ma,Guyang Cao,Jelena Diakonikolas,Ilias Diakonikolas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Massart噪声下学习漂移概念的问题，针对间隔可分离线性分类器（半空间）给出了一个计算高效的在线学习算法，其误差率为 η + Õ(Δ^{1/3}/γ)，并在下界方面通过低次多项式检验证明了 Δ^{1/3} 的缩放是不可避免的，表明了信息-计算折衷的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在Massart噪声环境下，目标概念随时间漂移的在线学习问题的复杂度，特别是针对间隔可分离线性分类器这一基础类别。

Method: 提出了一个计算高效的在线学习算法，该算法在Massart噪声和概念漂移的双重挑战下，实现了 η + Õ(Δ^{1/3}/γ) 的误差率；在可实现设定下，通过技术改编进一步改进了先前工作的误差率。下界方面，利用低次多项式检验证明 Δ^{1/3} 缩放的不可避免性。

Result: 上界：误差率 η + Õ(Δ^{1/3}/γ)，计算高效；下界：信息论最优误差为 Δ^{1/2}，但低次多项式检验下 Δ^{1/3} 缩放不可避免，暗示信息-计算折衷。

Conclusion: 本文算法在计算效率与误差率之间达到了近乎最优的平衡，揭示了该问题中存在信息-计算折衷。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning a drifting concept in the presence of Massart noise. In this framework, an online learner has access to a history of independent samples whose labels are noisy versions of a target concept that may change from round to round. The goal is to output, in each round, a hypothesis with small prediction error. We study the complexity of this learning problem for the fundamental class of margin-separable linear classifiers (halfspaces). On the positive side, we give a computationally efficient learner achieving error $η+ \tilde O(Δ^{1/3}/γ)$, where $η$ upper bounds the Massart noise rate, $Δ$ is the drift rate, and $γ$ is the margin. Interestingly, in the realizable setting, an adaptation of our techniques yields an efficient learner with an improved error rate over prior work. On the lower-bound side, we provide formal evidence of an information-computation tradeoff, strongly suggesting that our algorithm's performance is essentially optimal. Specifically, while the information-theoretically optimal error scales with $Δ^{1/2}$, we prove that $Δ^{1/3}$-scaling is unavoidable for low-degree polynomial tests, even in the special case of random classification noise.

</details>


### [176] [COGENT: Continuous Graph Emulators with Neural Ordinary Differential Equations for Long-Term Physical Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11162)
*Zesheng Liu,Maryam Rahnemoonfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COGENT提出了一种基于神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）的连续图仿真器，用于不规则地理空间网格上的长期物理预测。它通过图基历史编码器编码系统状态和强迫场，初始化隐式连续动力学系统，实现对任意未来时刻的预测，并在冰盖模拟任务中展现出优于自回归图基线方法的长期稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图神经网络的物理仿真方法通常受限于固定的时间离散化，且自回归预测方式易导致误差累积，难以实现长期稳定的预测，也无法灵活查询任意时刻的系统状态。

Method: 1) 图基历史编码器：编码有限历史状态和强迫场，生成节点上下文向量；2) 隐式神经常微分方程：上下文向量初始化并条件化潜在ODE，由插值未来强迫和相对卷展时间驱动动力学；3) 残差解码器：将潜在轨迹映射回未来物理状态，实现直接多步预测；4) 滚动时域采样与渐进式调度策略：稳定训练过程。

Result: 在冰盖瞬态模拟数据集上，COGENT相比自回归图基线方法展现出更优的长期预测稳定性，能够有效抑制误差累积问题。

Conclusion: 连续图神经常微分方程为不规则地理空间网格上的可扩展物理预测提供了一种有前景的方法，特别适用于需要稳定长期预测和任意时刻状态查询的应用场景。

Abstract: In this work, we present COGENT, a continuous graph emulator with Neural Ordinary Differential Equations for long-term physical forecasting on irregular geospatial meshes. COGENT encodes a finite history of system states and associated forcing fields and external forcings with a graph-based history encoder, producing node-wise context vectors that capture both local spatial interactions and temporal evolution. These context vectors initialize and condition a latent Neural Ordinary Differential Equation whose dynamics are driven by interpolated future forcings and explicit relative rollout time. By modeling the forecast trajectory as a continuous latent dynamical system, COGENT can generate predictions at arbitrary future times rather than being restricted to a fixed temporal discretization. A residual decoder maps the resulting latent trajectories back to future physical states, enabling direct multi-step forecasting without repeatedly feeding predicted states back into the model. This formulation combines graph-based spatial representation, history-conditioned latent dynamics, and continuous-time rollout in a unified framework for mesh-based physical simulation emulation. In order to stabilize training with long-horizon supervision, we also propose effective rollout-horizon sampling and a progressive rollout-horizon scheduling strategy. We evaluate COGENT on transient ice-sheet simulations generated by the Ice-sheet and Sea-level System Model, demonstrating improved long-range stability over autoregressive graph baselines. These results suggest that continuous graph Neural ODEs provide a promising methodology for scalable physical forecasting on irregular geospatial meshes, particularly in applications that require stable long-horizon predictions and the ability to query system states at arbitrary times.

</details>


### [177] [Algorithmic and Minimax Complexities in Kernel Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11171)
*Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的MAIR框架和算法信息论视角，将GP-UCB与DEC/MAMS方法联系起来，提出了结合两者优势的保护性主算法，并证明在过参数化模型中算法复杂度的信息含量可能超过类最小最大或DEC认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-UCB和DEC方法表面上属于不同的理论体系，缺乏统一的对比框架，无法清晰理解各自优势及适用场景。

Method: 建立统一的MAIR框架，引入异质半正定算法先验，将GP-UCB的算法高斯过程先验与MAMS的鲁棒类包络纳入同一种算法信息语言进行分析。

Result: 提出结合GP-UCB与MAMS优势的保护性主算法；在核Bandit构造中证明算法复杂度在某些过参数化模型中比类最小最大或DEC认证更具信息量。

Conclusion: 算法信息与类最小最大系数回答不同的问题，可能导致不同的差距；核Bandit为这种区别提供了清晰的数学可见性。

Abstract: Gaussian-process upper confidence bound (GP-UCB) and decision-estimation-coefficient (DEC) methods may appear, at first sight, to belong to different theories. This paper places the two viewpoints in a common algorithmic-information language for frequentist RKHS bandits. GP-UCB fixes an algorithmic, rather than true, Gaussian-process prior and exploits realized-trajectory complexity together with computational tractability, whereas MAMS optimizes a robust class-wide MAIR/DEC envelope. Through the unified MAIR framework and heterogeneous positive-semidefinite algorithmic priors, we generalize both the GP-UCB analysis and the MAMS algorithm, propose a safeguarded master that combines their advantages, and provide a kernel-bandit construction showing that algorithmic complexity can be more informative than class-wide minimax or DEC certificates in overparameterized models. The resulting message is that algorithmic information and class-wide minimax coefficients answer different questions and can lead to different gaps; kernel bandits provide a clean setting in which this distinction becomes mathematically visible.

</details>


### [178] [Predicting Future Behaviors in Reasoning Models Enables Better Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11172)
*Evgenii Kortukov,Piotr Komorowski,Florian Klein,Paula Engl,Gabriele Sarti,Seong Joon Oh,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出未来探测控制生成（FPCG）方法，通过训练激活探针预测推理模型未来行为，实现文本层面的可控生成，相比传统激活干预方法显著减少输出质量下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有推理模型在部署时行为不可控，测试时干预方法（激活干预）虽能改变输出但常导致质量下降。现有干预隐式依赖于检测已生成文本中行为的内部特征，但这些特征是未来行为的不良预测器，因此不是自然的干预目标。

Method: 训练激活探针从中间推理步骤预测未来行为概率（64%-91%准确率），发现了一类不同于检测特征的"预测特征"。基于此提出FPCG方法：采样多个候选句子，根据探针预测的未来行为概率选择最优者。

Result: FPCG在几乎不降低输出质量的前提下实现行为控制，并在多个激活干预失效的评估任务中成功实现干预。

Conclusion: 区分检测特征与预测特征为控制推理模型行为提供了更精细的方法。

Abstract: Deployed large reasoning models (LRMs) often behave unexpectedly. Test-time steering controls LRM outputs by intervening on their hidden representations, but it can degrade output quality. We argue that prior steering work implicitly relies on internal features that detect behavior in already generated text. We show that these detection features are poor predictors of future behavioral outcomes, and thus not the natural intervention target. Instead, we train activation probes to predict future behavior likelihoods from intermediate reasoning steps. These probes predict the most likely behavior with 64%-91% accuracy, revealing a separate type of internal prediction features. Building on these prediction features, we introduce a text-level steering method, Future Probe Controlled Generation. FPCG samples multiple candidate sentences and chooses the best one according to a probe predicting the future behavior likelihood. This enables steering with almost no output quality degradation. FPCG also enables steering in several evaluations where activation steering fails. These results show that distinguishing detection and prediction features enables a more nuanced approach to controlling LRM behaviors.

</details>


### [179] [When to Align, When to Predict: A Phase Diagram for Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11190)
*Ilay Kamai,Hugues Van Assel,Aviv Regev,Hagai B. Perets,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一线性框架，系统分析了跨模态对齐（CA）与跨模态预测（CP）两种多模态表示学习范式的成功与失败条件。在尖峰信号加噪声模型下，推导出两种目标的分离比，揭示互补的失败模式，并构建了四象限相图（Both、CA only、CP only、Neither）。通过少量标注子样本的数据驱动方法，可在训练前定位数据集所属象限并选择最优目标。实验验证了非线性场景下的预测，包括跨模态训练有害的Neither区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对跨模态对齐和跨模态预测何时有效、何时失败的系统性理解，尤其在生物医学、天体物理等科学领域，异构仪器和多层级测量数据使从业者无法诊断为何标准方法不如使用最佳单模态，亟需一个可诊断问题并指导目标选择的框架。

Method: 构建统一线性框架，采用尖峰信号加噪声模型（含结构化跨模态干扰相关），推导对齐和预测目标的分离比；据此划分四类问题（Both/CA only/CP only/Neither）；提出数据驱动方法，利用小规模标注子样本估算信噪比，定位数据集在相图中的位置，无需跨模态训练即可推荐最优目标和预测方向。

Result: 在合成数据、立体视觉基准、图像-文本对以及真实天体物理数据上，实验结果验证了相图预测在非线性场景下的有效性，包括存在Neither区域（跨模态训练有害）的情况。

Conclusion: 本框架让从业者能够在投入训练前诊断多模态问题并选择正确目标，既避免盲目使用跨模态方法带来的性能损失，也为科学领域异构数据场景提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment (CA) and cross-modal prediction (CP) are the dominant paradigms for multimodal representation learning, yet there is no systematic understanding of when each succeeds, when each fails, and when cross-modal training helps at all -- a gap that leaves practitioners, especially in scientific domains like biomedicine or astrophysics, with heterogeneous instruments and multiple levels of organization and measurement, unable to diagnose why standard methods underperform the best single modality. We develop a unified linear framework that addresses both questions. Under a spiked signal-plus-noise model with structured cross-modal nuisance correlation, we derive separation ratios for both objectives that expose complementary failure modes: alignment whitens each modality and fails when nuisance is strongly correlated across views; prediction encodes whatever is cross-predictable through a one-sided whitening, with recovery governed by source-modality quality. The resulting phase diagram partitions multimodal problems into four regimes: Both, CA only, CP only, and Neither. We present a data-driven procedure to locate real-world datasets in this diagram using a small labeled subsample, identifying the preferred objective and prediction direction before any cross-modal training. Experiments on synthetic data, stereo-vision benchmarks, image-caption pairs, and real astrophysical data validate the predictions in the nonlinear regime, including the Neither regime where cross-modal training is actively harmful. Our framework lets practitioners diagnose their multimodal problem and choose the right objective before committing to training. Code to reproduce the results is available at https://github.com/IlayMalinyak/mm_align_vs_pred.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Hardware-accelerated Aggregation: Unification and Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10030)
*Alireza Shateri,Hongshi Tan,Michael Ng,Bingsheng He,Qizhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据聚合操作在CPU、GPU和FPGA三种硬件平台上的加速方案，提出了统一硬件加速框架及硬件特定优化，并评估了性能和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域专用硬件的高效率推动了数据分系统中加速器的采用，GPU和FPGA是两种流行的云数据中心加速方案，但缺乏对聚合操作在多种硬件平台上系统性的比较和优化研究。

Method: 实现了一个统一的硬件加速框架用于聚合操作，该框架以牺牲部分效率换取易编程性和可移植性；在此基础上进一步开发了针对特定硬件（GPU和FPGA）的优化方案。

Result: 在CPU、GPU和FPGA三种平台上进行了评估，度量指标涵盖设备端和端到端处理的性能及能耗。

Conclusion: 论文通过统一的加速框架和硬件特定优化，系统比较了三种主流硬件平台在聚合操作上的表现，为数据分系统选择硬件加速方案提供了参考。

Abstract: The high efficiency of domain-specific hardware has sparked substantial interest in adopting accelerators in data analytics systems. Among many choices, GPUs and FPGAs thrived as two popular solutions due to their prevalent deployments in cloud data centers. This paper investigates hardware acceleration solutions for aggregation, a critical data analytics operation. Specifically, we implement aggregation with a unified hardware acceleration framework, which trades efficiency for ease of programming and portability, and then further develop hardware-specific optimizations. We evaluate these solutions on three recent computing hardware platforms: a CPU, a GPU, and an FPGA, with metrics that cover both the performance and energy consumption of on-device and end-to-end processing.

</details>


### [181] [RATrain: A Resource-Aware Training Runtime for Large Language Models on Bandwidth-Constrained Heterogeneous Supercomputing Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10415)
*Yao Lu,Shiqing Ma,Zhongzhi Luan,Gen Li,Jiaxing Qi,Bin Han,Hailong Yang,Depei Qian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RATrain是一种面向带宽受限异构超算平台的资源感知训练运行时系统，通过将1F1B训练建模为状态生命周期调度问题，结合MT-3000感知的执行后端和资源感知规划器，在显式内存层次结构和有限DDR容量约束下实现高效LLM训练，最高获得1.35倍加速比，在1024集群上达到97.0%扩展效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向GPU的训练运行时依赖高带宽设备内存、快速互连和成熟的集合通信库，难以直接适配MT-3000这类具有显式内存层次结构、可用DDR容量有限且集群间通信受限的异构超算平台。

Method: 将标准非交错1F1B训练建模为训练状态生命周期调度问题，在层级和阶段局部粒度上调度梯度同步、参数更新、参数视图预取和激活恢复；结合MT-3000感知的执行后端（高效FP16 GEMM、Attention Backward和显式数据移动）与资源感知规划器（在每计算集群20GB可用DDR约束下选择可行训练配置）。

Result: 在真实MT-3000平台上，使用LLaMA-2-7B、Baichuan2-13B、Qwen2.5-32B和LLaMA-2-70B配置评估，相比适配版GPU策略最高获得1.35倍端到端加速；LLaMA-2-7B扩展到1024集群，达112,790.55 tokens/s，扩展效率97.0%；1.028B token正确性验证最大相对损失偏差0.081%。

Conclusion: RATrain有效解决了带宽受限异构超算平台上稠密LLM训练的资源感知调度问题，在MT-3000上实现了显著性能提升和高扩展效率，同时保持了训练正确性。

Abstract: Production heterogeneous supercomputing platforms are increasingly used to host large language model (LLM) training workloads. However, existing GPU-oriented training runtimes typically rely on high-bandwidth device memory, fast interconnects, and mature collective communication libraries, making them difficult to directly adapt to MT-3000, a platform with an explicit memory hierarchy, limited usable DDR capacity, and constrained inter-cluster communication. This paper presents RATrain, a resource-aware training runtime for dense LLMs on bandwidth-constrained heterogeneous supercomputing platforms. RATrain formulates standard non-interleaved 1F1B training as a training-state lifecycle scheduling problem, and schedules gradient synchronization, parameter update, parameter-view prefetching, and activation recovery at layer-level and stage-local granularity. RATrain further combines an MT-3000-aware execution backend for efficient and predictable FP16 GEMM, Attention Backward, and explicit data movement with a resource-aware planner that selects feasible training configurations under the 20GB usable-DDR constraint per compute cluster. We implement RATrain on a real MT-3000 platform and evaluate it using LLaMA-2-7B, Baichuan2-13B, Qwen2.5-32B, and LLaMA-2-70B configurations. Results show that RATrain achieves up to 1.35$\times$ end-to-end speedup over MT-3000-adapted GPU-style training strategies. For LLaMA-2-7B, RATrain scales to 1024 compute clusters, reaches 112,790.55 tokens/s, and achieves 97.0\% scaling efficiency. A further 1.028B-token correctness run shows that RATrain preserves the loss trajectory of a semantically equivalent Baseline-1F1B run, with a maximum relative loss deviation of 0.081\%.

</details>


### [182] [ASTRA-sim 3.0: Next-Level Distributed Machine Learning Simulations via High-Fidelity GPU and Infrastructure Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10440)
*William Won,Jinsun Yoo,Tuan Ta,Moumita Dey,Andy Balogh,Pradosh Datta,Furkan Eris,Conor Green,Winston Liu,Changhai Man,Kingshuk Mandal,Amos Rai,Vinay Ramakrishnaiah,Ruchi Shah,David Sidler,Harsh Sikhwal,Hanjiang Wu,Tushar Krishna,Bradford M. Beckmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文改进了开源分布式ML模拟器ASTRA-sim，提出了3.0版本，通过缓存行级精细模拟、详细GPU执行模型和标准化基础设施表示InfraGraph，实现了高保真度仿真，支持大规模设计空间探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASTRA-sim模拟器在保真度和基础设施表示方面存在局限性，无法准确建模延迟敏感的集体通信，需要更精细的仿真能力和标准化的基础设施表示。

Method: 采用缓存行大小的加载-存储粒度仿真，集成详细GPU执行模型以平衡仿真规模与保真度；引入InfraGraph标准化表示来详细描述分布式ML网络基础设施。

Result: 通过ASTRA-sim 3.0模拟器，展示了在优化集体通信算法、网络需求和GPU架构方面的设计空间探索成果。

Conclusion: 高保真度仿真和标准化基础设施表示对于分布式ML系统设计至关重要，ASTRA-sim 3.0为未来的设计空间探索提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Distributed machine learning (ML) is a key paradigm for today's large-scale artificial intelligence applications. As model inference arises as an important use case, faithful modeling of latency-sensitive collective communication has never been more important. Capturing the device architecture and modeling control and data paths at high fidelity is therefore a necessity today. Having a common, detailed representation for distributed ML infrastructure is also crucial. We revisit the promising open-source, community-driven simulator: ASTRA-sim. In this work, we identify limitations of the current ASTRA-sim simulator and augment it with new features. To this end, we enable fine-grained, high-fidelity simulation with a standardized infrastructure representation, opening new design space exploration opportunities. We propose the simulation at cache-line-sized load-store granularity, with a detailed graphics processing unit (GPU) execution model, to balance simulation scalability and fidelity. We also introduce InfraGraph, a standardized representation to capture distributed ML network infrastructure in detail. Using the updated ASTRA-sim 3.0 simulator, we showcase interesting design space explorations for designing optimized collective algorithms, network requirements, and GPU architectures.

</details>


### [183] [Achieving Cloud-Grade SLOs for Local Mixture-of-Experts Inference through CPU-GPU Hybrid Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10493)
*Wenxin Wang,Yule Hou,Yu Ji,Peng Qu,Youhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种CPU-GPU混合推理系统，通过流式预填充加载、分布式预填充、预填充-解码分离、AVX-512优化的FP8 GEMV内核和细粒度CPU并行等五项技术，在双路商用CPU和消费级GPU上实现了云级服务质量的MoE模型本地部署，支持无损原始精度推理，吞吐量达1200-1800 tokens/s，解码速度达21.5-28 tokens/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署大型MoE模型在服务质量上远不及云环境，存在四大关键差距：依赖容量缩减模型、长预填充无法满足30秒TTFT、解码吞吐量低于基线、混合工作负载下并发性差。作者希望在不依赖数据中心基础设施的情况下，实现高质量、低成本的本地MoE推理。

Method: (1) 流式预填充加载（SLP）提升预填充吞吐量至1200 tokens/s；(2) 结合SmallEP专家并行的分布式SLP（DSLP）在两块RTX 5090上达1800 tokens/s；(3) 节点内预填充-解码分离，采用零拷贝共享权重和双批注意-MoE重叠机制；(4) AVX-512优化的FP8 GEMV内核，实现原生CPU FP8推理，延迟降低4-5倍；(5) 细粒度CPU并行，INT4 DeepSeek-V3达28 tokens/s，FP8 V3达21.5 tokens/s。

Result: SLP使预填充吞吐量达1200 tokens/s，32K提示可在30秒内完成；DSLP在双RTX 5090上达1800 tokens/s，45K提示在30秒内完成；预填充-解码分离方案延迟增加低于15%，吞吐量提升50%；FP8 GEMV内核降低CPU延迟4-5倍；INT4 DeepSeek-V3解码达28 tokens/s，FP8 V3解码达21.5 tokens/s。

Conclusion: 该CPU-GPU混合系统在消费级硬件上实现了旗舰MoE模型的云级服务质量，为本地部署提供了无损原始精度推理方案，使高质量、低成本的AI服务不再依赖数据中心基础设施。

Abstract: Local deployment of large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models falls short of the service quality achieved in cloud-scale environments, even under low-concurrency workloads. We identify four key gaps in local MoE inference: reliance on capacity-reduced models (quantized, distilled, rerouted), inability to meet 30-second TTFT for long prefills (more than 12K), sub-baseline decode throughput (under 20 tokens/s), and poor concurrency under mixed prefill-decode and batched decode workloads. We present a CPU-GPU hybrid system that achieves cloud-level SLOs on dual-socket commodity CPUs and consumer GPUs by (1) stream-loading prefill (SLP), boosting prefill throughput to 1,200 tokens/s and enabling 32K prompts within 30 seconds; (2) distributed SLP (DSLP) with SmallEP expert parallelism, reaching 1,800 tokens/s and 45K prompts in 30 seconds on two RTX 5090s; (3) intra-node prefill-decode disaggregation with zero-copy shared weights and a dual-batch attention-MoE overlap scheme, sustaining concurrency with under 15 percent latency increase and 50 percent throughput gains; (4) an AVX-512-optimized FP8 GEMV kernel, enabling native CPU FP8 inference while delivering 4-5x lower CPU latency; and (5) fine-grained CPU parallelism that attains 28 tokens/s on INT4 DeepSeek-V3 and 21.5 tokens/s on intact FP8 V3.
